    DQMC 数据在 (β=1/T=6)、系统尺寸 (L=16) 下提取。图 3 说明：*"All data are extracted from unbiased DQMC simulations at an inverse temperature (β=1/T=6) with system size (L=16)."* (ǎrepsilon) 使 charge-rich 与 hole-rich 亚晶格密度从 (h̊o=1) 线性偏离：*"The (ǎrepsilon) drives a linear deviation from half-filling ((h̊o=1)) on charge-rich and hole-rich sublattices."*

    • (U) suppresses charge inhomogeneity

    Hubbard 排斥倾向于单占据，因此抑制 USEP 引起的电荷不均匀。原文为：*"However, (U) favors single-electron occupation, thereby suppressing this charge inhomogeneity."*

    • DQMC spin structure factor peaks at ((π,0))

    动量空间自旋结构因子 (S(k)) 在 ((π,0)) 处出现尖峰，证明主导磁构型是条纹 SDW。原文为：*"The emergence of a sharp peak at (k=(π,0)) provides compelling evidence for the dominant spin-stripe configuration."*

    • DQMC reproduces (U)-(ǎrepsilon) competition

    (ǎrepsilon) 稀释局域磁矩并抑制 (S(π,0))，(U) 则显著增强条纹序。原文为：*"There reflects a competition that (ǎrepsilon) dilutes the local magnetic moments and suppresses (S(π,0)), whereas (U) dramatically facilitates it and drives the spin-stripe order."*

    • Finite-size scaling is the strongest evidence for finite-temperature SOAM

    SOAM 的核心证据是电荷不均匀背景下存在长程 SDW 序。原文为：*"The most compelling evidence for the stabilization of the SOAM phase is the existence of long-range SDW order with the charge inhomogeneity."*

    • Sign problem limits lower temperatures and larger sizes

    NNN 跃迁破坏半填充粒子-空穴对称性，符号问题在低温和大尺寸下变严重。原文为：*"the inclusion of NNN hoppings breaks the particle-hole symmetry at half-filling and introduces the sign problem that becomes severe at lower temperatures (T) and larger system sizes (L)."*

    • Low temperature enhances size dependence of (Oₛ)

    高温小 (β) 时，热涨落主导，(Oₛ) 几乎无尺寸依赖；降温后，(Oₛ) 随 (L) 单调增加，指向长程条纹序。原文为：*"In the high-(T) regime (small (β)), (Oₛ) is dominated by thermal fluctuations and exhibits negligible (L)-dependence."* 以及：*"As (T) is lowered, a pronounced finite-size effect emerges: (Oₛ) increases monotonically with (L)."*

    • (U=4) shows stronger stripe tendency than (U=3)

    (U=3) 时有限尺寸效应较弱；(U=4) 时 (Oₛ) 及其尺寸依赖在降温时更明显增强。原文为：*"This finite-size effect remains relatively weak for (U=3)"*，而：*"it reveal a more pronounced growth in both (Oₛ) and its (L)-dependence with decreasing (T) for (U=4)."*

    • Standard scaling form identifies long-range order

    有限尺寸标度采用

    [

    Oₛ/L²⁼a+b/L+c/L².

    ]

    原文为：*"Employing the standard scaling hypothesis (Oₛ/L²⁼a+b/L+c/L²), we extract (Oₛ/N) in the thermodynamic limit ((1/L→0))."* 有限截距表示长程序存在：*"A finite intercept indicates the establishment of long-range order."*

    • At (U=4.0), small (ǎrepsilon) supports long-range order; (ǎrepsilon=0.6) destroys it

    固定 (U=4.0) 时，小 (ǎrepsilon) 的标度曲线外推到有限截距，(ǎrepsilon=0.6) 截距消失。原文为：*"For a fixed (U=4.0), the scaling curves at small (ǎrepsilon) explicitly extrapolate to finite intercepts."* 以及：*"When (ǎrepsilon) is increased to 0.6, this intercept vanishes, denoting the breaking of long-range order by charge inhomogeneity."*

    • At (ǎrepsilon=0.4), (U=4.0) is sufficient but (U=3.0,3.5) are not

    固定 (ǎrepsilon=0.4) 时，(U=3.0)、(3.5) 不足以建立条纹相；(U=4.0) 出现清晰有限截距，且随 (U) 增强。原文为：*"At a fixed (ǎrepsilon=0.4), (U=3.0) and 3.5 are insufficient to host the stripe phase, while a clear finite intercept emerges at (U=4.0) and is further enhanced by (U)."*

    ---

    Conclusions

    • AM is extended beyond conventional AFM

    目标是探索传统 AFM 范式之外、由自旋不稳定性产生的 AM。结论开头为：*"To explore AM arising from spin instabilities beyond the conventional AFM paradigm, we investigate a minimal Hubbard model relevant to iron pnictides using Hartree-Fock MF and DQMC methods."*

    • Model retains essential iron-pnictide ingredients

    模型抓住了费米面拓扑和 ((π,0)) SDW 不稳定性。原文为：*"This model captures the essential Fermi surface topology and the ((π,0)) SDW instability."*

    • Charge inhomogeneity plus (U) generates (dₓy)-wave SOAM

    在 (ǎrepsilon) 引入电荷不均匀时，(U) 自发驱动 (dₓy)-波 SOAM，源于 SDW 不稳定性。原文为：*"with a charge inhomogeneity introduced by (ǎrepsilon), (U) spontaneously drive a novel (dₓy)-wave SOAM phase emerging from the SDW instability."*

    • SOAM combines real-space and momentum-space anisotropy

    该相同时具有实空间自旋条纹纹理和动量空间自旋劈裂的强各向异性。原文为：*"which integrates high anisotropy in both real-space spin texture and momentum-space spin splitting."*

    • Symmetry replacement is the mechanism for spin splitting

    电荷不均匀性使原本的时间反演-平移联合对称性破缺，并由时间反演-镜面联合不变性替代。原文为：*"the original combined time-reversal and translational symmetry is broken and replaced by a combined time-reversal and mirror invariance, which unlocks a pronounced nonrelativistic spin splitting."*

    • SOAM is broad but requires coexistence of competing tendencies

    (ǎrepsilon) 控制 AM 对称性，(U) 驱动条纹序；两者竞争但可在宽广区域共存。原文为：*"Although there is a competition between the (ǎrepsilon)-governed AM symmetry and the (U)-driven spin stripe, their remarkable coexistence stabilizes a broad SOAM insulating phase."*

    • DQMC finite-size scaling establishes finite-temperature stability

    无偏 DQMC 的有限尺寸标度表明 SOAM 可在可达有限温度下稳定建立。原文为：*"our exact finite-size scaling from unbiased DQMC simulations demonstrate that the SOAM phase can be stably established even at accessible finite temperatures."*

    • Model limitation is explicitly acknowledged

    模型不试图囊括真实铁基砷化物的完整微观复杂性，而聚焦费米面拓扑与磁性现象学。原文为：*"while our model does not capture the full microscopic complexity of real iron pnictides and focuses instead on the relevant Fermi surface topology and magnetic phenomenology."*

    • Cold-atom optical lattices are a promising platform

    USEP 可通过激光干涉工程化，因此光晶格冷原子系统是实现 SOAM 的可调平台。原文为：*"given that the USEP can be readily engineered via laser interference, ultracold atomic systems in optical lattices emerge as a highly promising and tunable experimental platform for the realization of this novel SOAM phase."*

    ---

    Acknowledgements

    • Funding and computational resources

    支持来源包括 NSFC 项目 12474218、北京市自然科学基金 1242022 与 1252022。原文为：*"This work is supported by NSFC (12474218) and Beijing Natural Science Foundation (No. 1242022 and 1252022)."* 数值模拟在北京师范大学 HSCC 完成：*"The numerical simulations in this work were performed at the HSCC of Beijing Normal University."*

    ---

    第四部分：深度理解与语言支持 (Understanding & Nuance)

    1. 术语解析

    • Altermagnetism / 交替磁性

    指一种不同于铁磁和传统反铁磁的磁相：总磁矩为零，但能带中存在动量依赖自旋劈裂。微妙之处在于，AM 的自旋劈裂不是 SOC 导致的 Rashba/Dresselhaus 型劈裂，而是由磁序和晶体对称性共同产生的非相对论劈裂。文中核心定义为 *"combining a vanishing net magnetization with macroscopic spin splitting, notably in the absence of spin-orbit coupling."*

    • Spin-density-wave instability / 自旋密度波不稳定性

    指费米面嵌套或相互作用导致某个波矢 (Q) 的自旋响应发散或显著增强，最终形成空间调制磁序。这里的关键波矢是 ((π,0))，对应铁基砷化物中常见的条纹磁序，而非简单的 Néel ((π,π)) 反铁磁。

    • Stripe-ordered altermagnetism (SOAM) / 条纹有序交替磁性

    不是普通“条纹磁序”加上普通“交替磁性”的简单叠加，而是：条纹 SDW 负责提供零净磁化的自旋背景，USEP 负责改变对称性，使该背景能够产生 AM 自旋劈裂。没有 (ǎrepsilon)，条纹 SDW 仍然自旋简并；没有 (U)，(ǎrepsilon) 也不能产生磁性劈裂。

    • Uniaxial staggered electric potential (USEP) / 单轴交错电势

    在四亚晶格上施加符号交错的电势：(l=1,2) 与 (l=3,4) 的电势相反。其物理效果不是直接产生磁性，而是产生 charge-rich / hole-rich 亚晶格，改变磁结构的空间对称性。

    • Nonrelativistic spin splitting / 非相对论自旋劈裂

    指不依赖自旋轨道耦合的自旋能带分裂。普通非磁晶体中，若有时间反演和反演对称性，Kramers 简并会保护自旋简并；AM 中，特定磁空间群破坏某些联合对称性，使 (Eₚ̆ₐᵣᵣₒw(k)≠ Edₒwₙₐᵣᵣₒw(k))，但总磁化仍为零。

    2. 疑难查证

    • 为什么纯条纹 SDW 不是 AM？

    纯条纹 SDW 有上、下自旋交替排列，且总磁矩为零，看起来很像 AM。但它保留 (T_dT)：先做时间反演把上自旋变下自旋，再沿对角方向平移 (T_d)，可以把系统映回自身。这个联合对称性把 (p̆arrow) 与 (downarrow) 能带配对起来，因此自旋能带不能分裂。也就是说，零净磁化和共线磁序不足以保证 AM；必须缺少保护自旋简并的联合对称性。

    • 为什么加入 (ǎrepsilon) 后反而能产生自旋劈裂？

    (ǎrepsilon) 不是磁场，不直接偏向上自旋或下自旋。它改变的是亚晶格电荷环境：某些位置 charge-rich，某些位置 hole-rich。原先通过对角平移可互相对应的两个位置现在不再等价，因此 (T_dT) 被破坏。与此同时，镜面对称 (M) 与时间反演 (T) 的组合仍能连接某些相反自旋亚晶格。这个新的 (MT) 对称结构允许某些 (k) 区域出现自旋劈裂，但仍强制某些高对称线或节点保持简并，从而形成 (dₓy)-波形态。

    • 为什么 (ǎrepsilon) 同时“需要”又“有害”？

    (ǎrepsilon) 对 AM 是必要的，因为它提供破坏 (T_dT)、保留 (MT) 的对称性条件；但它对 SDW 有害，因为电荷不均匀会稀释局域磁矩，削弱条纹磁序。于是 SOAM 只能存在于中等 (ǎrepsilon) 和足够 (U) 的窗口中。文中 (U=4.0) 时 (ǎrepsilon_capprox0.6) 会完全抑制条纹序，就是这种竞争的直接体现。

    • 为什么 (U) 增大反而会略微抑制自旋劈裂？

    (U) 的主要作用是促进局域单占据和磁序，因此它帮助形成条纹 SDW；但自旋劈裂大小依赖 (ǎrepsilon) 诱导的电荷不均匀性。较大 (U) 会惩罚双占据和电荷偏离，倾向把每个亚晶格拉回 (h̊oapprox1)，从而削弱 (ǎrepsilon) 的电荷调制效果。因此 (U) 对“是否有条纹磁序”是正作用，对“电荷不均匀导致的 AM 劈裂强度”可能是负作用。

    ---

    第五部分：创新评估与关联 (Synthesis & Novelty)

    1. 创新性判断

    • 从传统 AFM-AM 转向 SDW-AM

    过去 2–5 年 AM 研究主线集中在共线反铁磁材料、晶体对称性筛选、显式各向异性工程、具体材料候选与实验 ARPES 自旋劈裂观测。该工作的新颖点是把 AM 的起源从“已有反铁磁基态 + 合适晶体对称性”推进到“电子关联诱导的 SDW 不稳定性 + 电荷调制改变对称性”。

    • 解决“关联能否自发驱动 AM”问题的一种具体机制

    许多已有 Hubbard 或模型研究需要预设晶格/轨道各向异性，而这可能引入阻挫并削弱关联磁序。这里的机制更细：(U) 不是直接产生 AM 劈裂，而是先产生 ((π,0)) 条纹 SDW；(ǎrepsilon) 只负责破坏保护简并的 (T_dT) 并保留 (MT)。两者结合产生 SOAM。这回答了“电子关联能否通过非传统 AFM 路径参与 AM 建立”的问题。

    • 提出 (dₓy)-波条纹有序 AM 这一具体相

    自旋劈裂分布集中在布里渊区对角线，并在特定高对称路径上有节点，构成非常明确的 (dₓy)-波 AM 指纹。这不是泛泛的“存在自旋劈裂”，而是给出可诊断的动量空间纹理。

    • 同时使用 MF 与无偏 DQMC 增强可信度

    MF 给出机制、相图和能带劈裂；DQMC 在 (β=6)、有限尺寸标度中验证条纹长程序可在有限温和涨落下保留。相对纯均场理论，DQMC 结果显著提高了强关联结论的可靠性。

    • 把铁基砷化物 SDW 物理与 AM 连接起来

    铁基砷化物长期以 ((π,0)) 条纹 SDW、(Γ) 空穴口袋和 (X/Y) 电子口袋为核心物理。该工作指出这些传统 SDW 结构可成为 AM 的母体平台，为铁基体系中的 AM 搜索提供新方向。

    • 实验平台从固体材料扩展到冷原子光晶格

    真实铁基材料中的 USEP 工程可能复杂，但冷原子光晶格可通过激光干涉构造交错电势和调节 Hubbard 参数。因此该机制不仅是材料启发模型，也具有可控量子模拟路线。

    阅读原文 ↗
    原文链接页内定位
  12. 12
    CrSb 中三维体分辨的 g 波交替磁序参量#
    3D bulk-resolved g-wave altermagnetic order parameter in CrSb#
    📊 含图深析
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Mengmeng Long, Theodore I. Weinberger, Zheyu Wu, Mads F. Hansen, Ran Tao, Mridul Shrestha, Dave Graf, Yurii Skourski, F. Malte Grosche, Alexander G. Eaton🔥 AI 1

    📄 摘要：在 CrSb 中解析出三维体分辨的 g 波交替磁序参量，显示其自旋分裂具有明确的体相与节点结构，可作为检测非常规磁对称破缺的实验基准。

    💡 亮点：CrSb 的 g 波交替磁序参量被做成三维体分辨。它把抽象的节点型磁序参数变成可实验检验的体相信号。

    📖 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题如何在金属 CrSb 的体相中直接证明并三维解析交替磁自旋劈裂序参量？核心是判断自旋上、下费米面是否沿特定动量方向简并、沿低对称方向分裂，并确认其节点面是否构成 g-wave 型角向图案。
    创新方法用体敏感 de Haas–van Alphen 量子振荡作为“动量空间切片仪”：旋转磁场穿过节点面、反节点面和低对称倾斜平面，观察单一频率何处分裂成双峰、何处重新合并，从而把不可见的交换劈裂 Δ(k) 映射成三维 g-wave 节点图；Aha 点是用量子振荡频率分裂/坍缩直接画出 𝒴₄⁻³ = yz(3x² − y²) 型磁序参量。
    工作流程输入高质量 CrSb 单晶，在 0.4 K 下施加强磁场并旋转方向；悬臂梁磁扭矩记录量子振荡波形；FFT 提取频率峰，节点方向出现单峰，反节点和低对称方向出现双峰；结合 DFT 费米面模拟、Onsager 关系和 Lifshitz–Kosevich 有效质量拟合；最终输出 dogbone 三维空穴口袋的自旋劈裂费米面、节点面位置和 g-wave 序参量对称性。
    关键结果CrSb 主费米面是近似狗骨形三维空穴口袋；在 c–a 节点面内只有 4.1 kT 单一频率，在 c–ab 反节点面稍偏离高对称轴后单峰迅速裂成双峰，4° 旋转即可产生约 0.6 kT 频率差；低对称旋转中双分支在 φ = 0°、±60° 附近重新合并；有效质量 m₁* = 1.73(5)mₑ、m₂* = 1.84(5)mₑ，且 m₁*/m₂* = 0.94 ± 0.04 ≈ √(f₁/f₂) = 0.945；估算费米能级自旋劈裂约 25 meV；序参量符合 𝒴₄⁻³(θ,φ) ∝ P₄³(cosθ)sin3φ，属于 D₆h 点群 B₁g 表示。
    应用价值该研究把 CrSb 确立为清晰的室温以上金属 g-wave 交替磁体原型，并证明量子振荡可作为诊断金属交替磁体体相序参量的高分辨工具；CrSb 具有约 740 K 磁有序温度、低残余电阻率、空气稳定和元素易得等优势，可作为低能耗自旋电子学与非常规磁性器件的候选平台。

    第一部分：核心概览（Overview）

    CrSb 被确立为一种室温金属 g-wave 交替磁体（altermagnet）：其自旋上、下费米面在体相中发生动量依赖的非相对论交换劈裂，且劈裂序参量服从实球谐函数 𝒴₄⁻³ = yz(3x² − y²) 的对称性。核心突破在于利用体敏感三维量子振荡谱学直接追踪 CrSb 费米面在不同磁场方向下的自旋劈裂、节点面与反节点面，实现了非常规磁序参量的三维映射。该工作将量子振荡确立为诊断金属交替磁体体相序参量的高分辨工具，并使 CrSb 成为迄今最清晰的 g-wave 金属交替磁体原型材料之一。

    ---

    第二部分：结构化快速回顾

    《3D bulk-resolved g-wave altermagnetic order parameter in CrSb》（None，）

    背景

    交替磁体兼具反铁磁体的零净磁化与铁磁体式动量依赖自旋劈裂。CrSb 具有高达约 740 K 的磁有序温度、金属性和 NiAs 型六方结构，是重要候选材料。此前 RuO₂、KV₂Se₂O 等体系的表面实验与体相实验存在矛盾，凸显体相探测的必要性。

    方法

    采用de Haas–van Alphen 量子振荡和悬臂梁磁扭矩测量，在 0.4 K 下旋转磁场穿过 CrSb 布里渊区的节点面、反节点面和低对称倾斜平面；结合 DFT 模拟、FFT 频谱、Lifshitz–Kosevich 有效质量分析，重构自旋劈裂费米面。

    结果

    CrSb 的主费米面为近似狗骨形三维空穴口袋，在节点方向出现单一量子振荡频率，在反节点和低对称方向分裂为两条频率分支。典型频率包括 4.1 kT、3.6 kT、3.41 kT、3.82 kT；有效质量为 1.73(5) mₑ 与 1.84(5) mₑ；估算费米能级自旋劈裂约 25 meV。

    讨论

    自旋劈裂的节点结构包含三个方位角节点面 φ = 0°、60°、120° 和一个极角节点面 θ = 90°，与 𝒴₄⁻³(θ,φ) ∝ P₄³(cosθ) sin3φ 完全一致，对应 D₆h 点群的 B₁g 不可约表示。

    局限

    复杂的低频 web-like 费米面在 ≲2 kT 区域产生复杂角度依赖，使其自旋劈裂不如高频 dogbone 口袋清晰；实验主要聚焦于 ≳3 kT 的 dogbone 口袋。提供内容未包含完整样品数量、所有误差来源、完整 DFT 参数和统计显著性检验。

    结论

    CrSb 的体相电子结构呈现清晰的 g-wave 交替磁序参量，量子振荡可直接解析非常规金属磁体的三维序参量对称性。CrSb 兼具高有序温度、低残余电阻率、空气稳定性和元素可得性，是低能耗自旋电子学的重要候选平台。

    ---

    第三部分：逐章节冷凝解析（Section-by-Section Condensing）

    3D bulk-resolved g-wave altermagnetic order parameter in CrSb

    Electronic order parameters define broken symmetries

    电子相的本质由序参量刻画，磁性与超导均可通过自发对称性破缺来分类。传统例子包括铁磁体的均匀磁化和常规超导体的各向同性能隙。非常规超导中的节点能隙概念被推广到非常规磁体与交替磁体中，其中费米面附近自旋上、下能带不再简并。核心表述为：*"Electronic phases of matter, such as magnetism and superconductivity, are defined and distinguished by their order parameters quantifying the spontaneous symmetry breaking underlying each phase."* 以及 *"Unconventional superconductors often have a nodal gap function, where the gap changes sign at nodes on the Fermi surface."*

    Quantum oscillations provide bulk 3D order-parameter mapping

    磁量子振荡被用作体敏感、高分辨率、三维映射工具，直接解析非常规磁体的序参量。相比表面敏感的 ARPES，量子振荡反映体相费米面极值截面积，适合确认候选交替磁体的本征性质。关键声明为：*"Here we demonstrate that magnetic quantum oscillation measurements can provide a high resolution, bulk-sensitive, 3D mapping of the order parameter in an unconventional magnet."*

    CrSb shows symmetry-reduced spin-split Fermi sheets

    通过旋转磁场穿过 CrSb 布里渊区的高对称与低对称方向，发现每个自旋劈裂费米面在离开节点方向后对称性降低。实验解析的交换劈裂服从 𝒴₄⁻³ = zy(3x² − y²)，等价于氢原子的 g 轨道角动量对称性。原文证据为：*"By rotating a magnetic field through high- and low-symmetry directions of the CrSb Brillouin zone, we show that this material’s altermagnetic band structure leads to a reduction of symmetry for each spin-split Fermi sheet away from nodal orientations."* 以及 *"the exchange splitting between up and down spins follows the profile of the 𝒴₄⁻³ = zy(3x² − y²) real spherical harmonic – analogous to a g-orbital of the hydrogen atom."*

    CrSb is positioned as a prototypical g-wave metallic altermagnet

    g-wave 序参量在非常规超导中通常难以直接分辨，而 CrSb 中的磁性序参量可通过量子振荡准粒子谱精确映射，从而成为金属交替磁体的原型体系。关键结论为：*"our work demonstrates that the order parameter symmetry of unconventional magnets can be precisely mapped via quantum-oscillatory quasiparticle spectroscopy, establishing CrSb as a prototypical g-wave metallic altermagnet."*

    Altermagnetism combines antiferromagnetic compensation with ferromagnetic-like spin splitting

    交替磁体与反铁磁体一样具有交替自旋排列，但其自旋子晶格由旋转对称性连接，而非单纯平移或反演。这导致 Kramers 自旋简并解除和动量依赖非相对论自旋劈裂。核心定义为：*"Like antiferromagnets, altermagnets possess alternating up and down spin orientations from site to site."* 以及 *"the distinction is that in an altermagnet the spin sublattices are connected by rotational symmetries rather than just translation (or inversion)."* 进一步结果是：*"This leads to the lifting of Kramers spin degeneracy and a momentum-dependent non-relativistic spin-splitting of the electronic band structures."*

    Metallic room-temperature altermagnets are technologically important but experimentally delicate

    金属交替磁体若有远高于室温的有序温度，将利于电子输运和器件应用。CrSb、RuO₂、KV₂Se₂O 等曾被提出为候选体系，但 RuO₂ 与 KV₂Se₂O 的表面与体相结果出现冲突，因此体敏感实验证据非常关键。原文指出：*"The realisation of a metallic altermagnet, with an ordering temperature far above 300 K, would be particularly desirable for efficient electronic transport in technological device settings."* 并强调：*"These cautionary tales motivate bulk-sensitive studies of candidate altermagnets, in order to firmly establish their intrinsic magnetic properties."*

    ---

    Nodal magnetic order parameters

    Unconventional magnetic order parameters are defined by exchange splitting

    非常规磁体的序参量被定义为同一波矢处自旋上、下电子态的能量差：Δ(k) = E(k,↑) − E(k,↓)。该定义把超导能隙的节点结构类比推广到磁交换劈裂。原文定义为：*"Let us define the order parameter for an unconventional magnet as the exchange splitting (energy difference) between up- and down-spin states at a given wavevector, Δ(k)=E(k,↑)−E(k,↓)."*

    Spin-split Fermi sheets are separated by Δ(k)/(ℏvF)

    交换劈裂在费米面上表现为自旋上、下费米片之间的动量间隔，近似为 k⊥ ≃ Δ(k)/(ℏvF)，其中 vF = ∂E/(ℏ∂k)。这使量子振荡能够通过面积差反推出交换劈裂。原文给出：*"This produces spin-split Fermi sheets separated by the (perpendicular) wavevector k⊥ ≃ Δ(k)/(ℏvF), where vF = ∂E/(ℏ∂k) is the Fermi velocity."*

    Ferromagnets are analogous to s-wave BCS superconductors

    铁磁体具有近似各向同性的自旋劈裂，可类比常规 s-wave BCS 超导体；非常规磁体则类比 p/d/f/g-wave 超导，具有节点面与符号变化。原文表述为：*"a ferromagnet is analogous to a BCS superconductor – its nonzero magnetisation splits the Fermi surface into unequal majority and minority spin species, characterised by an isotropic energy gap between them."*

    p-, d-, f-, and g-wave magnetic orders have increasing numbers of nodal planes

    非常规磁序的角向对称性决定节点面数目：p/d/f/g-wave 分别对应 1/2/3/4 个高对称节点面；在节点面上自旋特征交换，交换劈裂为零或受对称性强制简并。关键句为：*"for an unconventional magnet of p/d/f/g-wave symmetry, the Fermi surface will possess 1/2/3/4 highly symmetric nodal planes at which up-character swaps to down, and vice versa."*

    CrSb crystal symmetry enforces screw-rotation-connected spin sublattices

    CrSb 属于六方 P6₃/mmc NiAs 型结构，Cr 原子沿 c 轴堆叠，Sb 原子形成三角棱柱式间隙排列；约 740 K 以下，Cr 磁矩沿 c 轴交替排列。Sb 的三角排列要求用 6₃ 螺旋旋转将自旋上 Cr 位点映射到自旋下 Cr 位点。原文细节为：*"CrSb crystallises in the hexagonal P6₃/mmc NiAs-type structure"*，*"Below an ordering temperature of around 740 K... the chromium sites possess alternating magnetic moments oriented along the c-axis"*，以及 *"The trigonal arrangement of antimony necessarily requires a 6₃ screw rotation to map a spin-up chromium site to its spin-down counterpart."*

    CrSb preserves a composite nonsymmorphic time-reversal symmetry

    CrSb 破坏纯时间反演与原胞平移对称性，但保留由非点式空间操作与时间反演组合而成的复合对称性。这一结构使其满足交替磁体条件 Δ(Gk) = −Δ(k)。原文证据为：*"while CrSb breaks both pure time-reversal and primitive lattice-translation symmetries, it preserves a combined symmetry pairing this non-symmorphic spatial operation with time reversal."* 以及 *"CrSb is a metallic altermagnet in which Δ(Gk)=−Δ(k), where G is this composite operation."*

    ---

    Quantum oscillation measurements

    Quantum oscillations reveal nodal planes through frequency degeneracy

    量子振荡可识别非常规磁体的节点面，因为当磁场位于节点面内时，自旋上、下费米面轨道可通过镜面对称互相映射，包围相同面积，因此每个费米口袋只产生一个量子振荡频率。原文解释为：*"Aligning a magnetic field within a nodal plane therefore produces QO orbits for up- and down-spin Fermi sheets that are different, but can be mapped onto each other by a mirror operation about the nodal plane, and which therefore enclose the same area."* 结果是：*"This leads to a single QO frequency per Fermi pocket for fields in nodal planes."*

    Off-nodal orientations produce two quantum-oscillation frequencies

    磁场离开节点面后，自旋上、下费米面不再由对称操作联系，通常包围不同截面积，产生两个不同 QO 频率。这是交替磁自旋劈裂的“烟枪”信号。原文表述为：*"for fields oriented away from nodal planes, the up- and down-spin Fermi sheets are not related by any symmetry operation, and therefore typically enclose different areas, leading to two distinct QO frequencies."* 并称其为：*"an expected smoking-gun signature of altermagnetic spin-splitting."*

    CrSb has specific nodal and antinodal rotation geometries

    角度定义为：方位角 φ 位于六方基面，从 a 到 ab；极角 θ 从 c 指向 ab 基面。对于六方 CrSb，a 与 b 等价；c–a 是高对称节点面，c–ab 是反节点面，其中自旋简并只在 c 与 ab 方向受对称性强制。原文为：*"The azimuthal angle φ is defined as the inclination from a to ab in the hexagonal basal plane; the polar angle θ is from c towards the basal ab plane."* 以及 *"The c−a plane is a highly symmetric nodal plane in which Kramers degeneracy is enforced by symmetry, while c−ab is antinodal, in which spin degeneracy is only symmetry-enforced at c and ab."*

    g-wave splitting predicts 60° azimuthal and 90° polar nodal periodicities

    CrSb 的 g-wave 劈裂预期在基面 a–ab 旋转中每 δφ = 60° 出现节点，在 c–ab 平面旋转中每 δθ = 90° 出现节点。原文明确给出：*"In CrSb, g-wave splitting should result in nodal planes every δφ=60° for rotations in the a−ab plane, and every δθ=90° rotating through the c−ab plane."*

    The primary Fermi pocket is a closed dogbone-like 3D hole pocket

    实验与图示中的主费米面为封闭三维椭球状、类似狗骨的空穴口袋。其节点面在自旋上、下片之间作为对称强制简并面。原文描述为：*"This sheet is a closed ellipsoid-like pocket of hole character, shaped like a dogbone."*

    Nodal c–a measurements show a single 4.1 kT frequency

    在 0.4 K 下，用悬臂梁磁扭矩测量背景扣除后的振荡扭矩 Δτ。当 H ∥ a，即 θ = 90°，φ = 0°，以及在 c–a 节点面内轻微偏转到 θ = 88°，φ = 0° 时，FFT 均显示单一频率峰 4.1 kT。原文细节为：*"The blue waveform was measured for H∥a at θ=90°, φ=0°"*，*"the orange data collected a small rotation away in the c−a plane to θ=88°, φ=0°"*，以及 *"the corresponding fast Fourier transform (FFT) spectra... exhibit a singular frequency peak at 4.1 kT."*

    ---

    Tracking Δ(k) = E(k,↑) − E(k,↓)

    Antinodal c–ab rotation reveals spin-split beat patterns

    在反节点 c–ab 平面，H ∥ ab（θ = 90°，φ = 90°）时仍为单频 3.6 kT；向 c 方向仅旋转 3° 后，波形出现明显拍频，FFT 中解析出两个峰，对应自旋劈裂的非简并费米面。原文证据为：*"The green curve... was obtained for H∥ab (i.e. θ=90°, φ=90°), with a monofrequency profile of 3.6 kT."* 以及 *"Upon a small θ rotation of 3° towards c, a distinct beat pattern emerges... with two peaks clearly resolved in the frequency spectra."*

    A 4° rotation produces 0.6 kT splitting

    在靠近 ab 方向的细致角度扫描中，θ 每次递增 1°，单一 3.6 kT 轴向峰迅速分裂为两条分支；到 4° 旋转时两峰相隔 0.6 kT。原文写道：*"Incrementing θ in 1° steps through the c−ab plane, we find that the singular on-axis peak of 3.6 kT clearly splits into two distinct frequency branches, which by 4° of rotation are separated by 0.6 kT."*

    Quantum-oscillation frequencies directly measure Fermi-surface areas

    QO 频率与垂直磁场方向的费米面极值截面积 𝒜⊥ 成正比，因此频率劈裂可直接解释为自旋上、下费米面横截面积不同。原文指出：*"QO frequencies are directly proportional to the Fermi surface cross-sectional area 𝒜⊥ normal to H."*

    DFT reproduces nodal and antinodal frequency patterns

    DFT 模拟与实验频谱对比显示：dogbone 口袋在 H ∥ a 时为 4.1 kT，在 H ∥ ab 时为 3.6 kT；另有复杂 web-like 电子型费米片，在低频 ≲2 kT 区域产生复杂角度依赖。原文细节为：*"The dogbone pocket exhibits QO frequencies of 4.1 kT for H∥a and 3.6 kT for H∥ab."* 以及 *"The web sheet produces a very complicated dependence of QO frequency on angle in the low frequency range ≲2 kT."*

    The dogbone pocket is the cleanest probe of altermagnetic splitting

    由于 web 口袋几何复杂，离开节点面后虽也会发生自旋劈裂，但难以解析；高频 ≳3 kT dogbone 轨道远离 web 背景，因此成为主要分析对象。原文说明：*"Away from nodal planes, the web is also split into up and down sheets, but its geometrical complexity makes this more difficult to resolve than for the dogbone."* 以及 *"We therefore predominantly focus on the dogbone... which produces QO orbits with frequencies ≳3 kT, well above those from the web."*

    Antinodal symmetry is reduced from 90° to 180°

    在节点平面中，自旋上、下频率角度轨迹每 90° 重复；在反节点平面中，单个自旋劈裂分支失去镜面对称性，周期降低为 180°，实验与模拟一致。原文为：*"whereas in the nodal plane both spin-up and spin-down frequency vs angle traces are expected to repeat every 90°, in the antinodal plane this reduces to every 180° – exactly as observed experimentally."*

    Low-symmetry tilted-plane rotation confirms 60° nodal recurrence

    为追踪远离节点面的自旋劈裂，实验采用相对样品架倾斜 14° 的低对称旋转平面。磁场方向与晶体坐标满足 cosθ = sin(14°)sinα 和 tanφ = cos(14°)tanα。原文给出：*"We experimentally performed this by first rotating the sample platform 14° orthogonally to the axis of the solenoid before loading it into the magnet."* 以及 *"cos(θ)=sin(14°)sin(α) and tan(φ)=cos(14°)tan(α)."*

    Low-symmetry data match DFT and collapse at φ = 0°, ±60°

    在低对称弧线上，除了节点面外自旋劈裂显著；在 φ = 0° 与 φ = ±60° 附近两支频率重新合并，符合 g-wave 节点面每 60° 出现的预期。原文证据为：*"The correspondence between experimental observation... and theoretical prediction... is very good."* 以及 *"Upon incrementing α, this then splits into two distinct branches, which, within resolution, come back together again close to φ=±60° as the next nodal plane is crossed, as expected for Δ(k) possessing g-wave symmetry."*

    ---

    Altermagnetic scaling of m↑*/m↓* = √(f↑/f↓)

    Temperature-dependent amplitudes yield two spin-split effective masses

    在低对称旋转平面 α = 22°，对应晶体坐标 θ = 84.8°，φ = 21.4°，测得两个清晰频率峰：f₁ = 3.41 kT 与 f₂ = 3.82 kT。通过 Lifshitz–Kosevich 温度阻尼拟合得到有效回旋质量 m₁* = 1.73(5)mₑ 与 m₂* = 1.84(5)mₑ。原文为：*"Two peaks are clearly resolved, with f₁ = 3.41 kT and f₂ = 3.82 kT."* 以及 *"Fitting the temperature-dependent oscillatory damping to Lifshitz-Kosevich theory yields effective masses m₁* = 1.73(5)me and m₂* = 1.84(5)me."*

    Mass and frequency ratios validate common parent Fermi sheet

    如果两个 QO 分支是同一母费米面的自旋劈裂子分支，它们应共享相同质量重整化和费米速度轮廓，因此满足 m₁*/m₂* = √(f₁/f₂)。实验得到 m₁*/m₂* = 0.94 ± 0.04，与 √(f₁/f₂) = 0.945 高度一致。原文关键证据为：*"For two QO frequency branches that are spin-split daughters of the same mother sheet, the daughters are expected to share the same mass renormalisation factors and Fermi velocity profile."* 以及 *"This is indeed what we observe: m₁*/m₂* = 0.94 ± 0.04 ≃ √(f₁/f₂) = 0.945."*

    The inferred Fermi-level spin splitting is about 25 meV

    由频率差 δf = f₂ − f₁ = 0.41 kT，结合 Onsager 关系和平均有效质量 m* ≃ 2.03mₑ，估算该磁场方向下费米能级自旋劈裂 Δ ≈ 25 meV。原文写道：*"The maximum spin-splitting can likewise be estimated from the frequency difference δf=f₂−f₁=0.41 kT"*，并给出结果：*"With the observed average effective mass m* ≃ 2.03me, we thereby estimate a spin-splitting at the Fermi level of Δ ≈ 25 meV for this field orientation."*

    Background torque retains crystal symmetry even when QO branches lose mirror symmetry

    原始扭矩背景在高对称方向整体挠曲较小，主要由振荡磁化贡献；即使反节点平面中的自旋分支频率角度轨迹失去镜面对称性，背景扭矩仍体现晶体结构的高对称性。原文为：*"Close to high-symmetry, the overall cantilever deflection is small, hence τ is dominated by the oscillatory component of magnetisation."* 以及 *"Although the QO frequency profiles for these two planes are markedly different... the background torque profile still exhibits the higher symmetry representative of the crystal structure."*

    ---

    g-wave magnetic order parameter

    Four nodal planes determine the magnetic order-parameter symmetry

    综合三个旋转平面，CrSb 主费米口袋的自旋劈裂频率演化确定了四类节点面：三个对所有 θ 有效的方位角节点面 φ = 0°、60°、120°，以及一个对所有 φ 有效的极角节点面 θ = 90°。原文总结为：*"we identify four nodal planes in total: three defined for all θ at φ=0°, 60°, 120°, and one for all φ at θ=90°."*

    The order parameter follows 𝒴₄⁻³ and belongs to B₁g of D₆h

    CrSb 的非常规磁序参量 Δ(θ,φ) 与实球谐函数 𝒴₄⁻³(θ,φ) ∝ P₄³(cosθ) sin3φ 对称性一致，并属于 CrSb D₆h 点群的 B₁g 不可约表示。原文为：*"We find that Δ(θ,φ) obeys the symmetries of 𝒴₄⁻³(θ,φ) ∝ P₄³(cosθ) sin3φ"*，以及 *"This harmonic transforms as the B₁g irreducible representation of the D₆h point group of CrSb."*

    Cartesian and spherical forms expose the g-wave node structure

    该实球谐函数可写为笛卡尔形式 yz(3x² − y²)，或球坐标形式 r⁴ sin³θ cosθ sin(3φ)。其节点位于 θ = 0°、90° 和 φ = 0°、60°、120°，与实验确定的 Δ(θ,φ) 节点一致。原文给出：*"𝒴₄⁻³(θ,φ) can be expressed in Cartesian form as yz(3x²−y²) or equivalently in spherical polars as r⁴ sin³(θ) cos(θ) sin(3φ)."* 以及 *"This function has nodes when θ=0°, 90° and φ=0°, 60°, 120°, just as Δ(θ,φ) does."*

    Quantum oscillations are established as diagnostics of unconventional metallic magnetism

    量子振荡对自旋劈裂能带极其敏感，可直接确定非常规金属磁体的序参量对称性。原文指出：*"Our study thereby demonstrates the sensitivity of QO measurements to spin-split band structures, positioning such measurements as an ideally suited diagnostic tool for ascertaining the order parameter symmetry of unconventional metallic magnets."*

    Nodal planes produce spin-degenerate mirror-symmetric quasiparticle orbits

    当磁场位于节点面时，Δ(k) 为零，自旋轨道镜面对称且 QO 频率简并；当磁场穿过低对称弧线时，Δ(k) 通常很大并可解析，经过节点面时重新坍缩为零。原文总结为：*"For magnetic fields applied in highly symmetric nodal planes, Δ(k) is always zero – whereas, rotating through arcs of low symmetry Δ(k) is generally large and well-resolvable, collapsing back to zero upon crossing through a nodal plane."* 以及 *"For fields applied in nodal planes, quasiparticle orbits are spin-degenerate and mirror-symmetric."*

    Antinodal loss of mirror symmetry is the hallmark of altermagnetic order

    反节点平面中，单个自旋费米面轨道失去镜面对称性，导致 QO 频率-角度轨迹分裂，这是交替磁有序的标志。原文表述为：*"By contrast, in antinodal planes this mirror symmetry is lost, leading to spin-split QO frequency versus angle traces – providing a hallmark signature of altermagnetic ordering."*

    CrSb has strong application-relevant materials properties

    CrSb 晶体质量高，残余电阻率最低达到 2 μΩ cm；材料对空气和湿气相对不敏感，可高纯合成；交替磁有序温度很高；Cr 与 Sb 元素相对丰富易得。这些条件使其成为低能耗自旋电子学候选材料。原文为：*"our optimised growth procedure has produced high-quality crystals with residual resistivities as low as 2 μΩ cm."* 以及 *"Given that CrSb is relatively insensitive to air and moisture, it can be synthesised to high purity, the altermagnetic ordering temperature is very high, and both chromium and antimony are relatively abundant easily-sourced elements..."*

    ---

    第四部分：深度理解与语言支持（Understanding & Nuance）

    1. 术语解析

    Altermagnetism（交替磁性）

    不是普通反铁磁，也不是弱铁磁。关键在于自旋上、下子晶格由旋转对称性而非单纯平移/反演联系，因此虽然净磁化为零，却可以产生类似铁磁体的动量依赖自旋劈裂。微妙点：交替磁体的“spin splitting”通常是非相对论起源，不依赖 Rashba 型自旋轨道耦合。

    Kramers spin degeneracy（Kramers 自旋简并）

    通常由时间反演对称性保证：无磁场、半整数自旋体系中，k 与 −k 态成对简并。交替磁体中纯时间反演被破坏，某些方向仍因复合对称性保持简并，另一些方向则发生劈裂。文中的重点不是所有 k 点都劈裂，而是存在受对称性保护的节点面。

    Nodal plane（节点面）

    指序参量 Δ(k) 因对称性为零或自旋上、下轨道面积相等的高对称平面。与非常规超导的节点相似，但这里不是超导能隙为零，而是交换劈裂为零或量子振荡频率简并。

    Antinodal plane（反节点面）

    与节点面相对，代表序参量通常达到较大值、对称性不再强制简并的方向。CrSb 的 c–ab 平面就是反节点面；在那里，稍微偏离高对称轴后 QO 单峰迅速分裂成两峰。

    Real spherical harmonic 𝒴₄⁻³（实球谐函数 𝒴₄⁻³）

    球谐函数描述角向对称性。这里不是说电子真的处于氢原子 g 轨道，而是说自旋交换劈裂在动量空间中的角分布与 g 轨道型函数 yz(3x²−y²) 具有相同节点结构和符号变化。

    ---

    2. 疑难查证

    为什么量子振荡能看见交替磁序参量？

    量子振荡频率满足 Onsager 关系：频率正比于垂直磁场方向的费米面极值截面积。若自旋上、下费米面完全简并，只有一个频率；若交替磁交换劈裂使两者面积不同，就会出现两个频率。旋转磁场相当于从不同方向“切”费米面，逐步扫描自旋劈裂的角向分布。节点面内两个自旋轨道由镜面对称相关，面积相同；低对称方向失去这种关系，面积不同，频率分裂。

    为什么频率分裂不是简单 Zeeman 劈裂？

    Zeeman 劈裂由外磁场直接导致，通常随磁场大小变化；这里关注的是零场磁序导致的内禀动量依赖交换劈裂，由晶体和磁结构对称性规定。关键证据是频率分裂对角度具有严格节点结构：在 φ = 0°、60°、120° 和 θ = 90° 等对称方向坍缩，而在低对称方向显著出现。这种角向符号与节点结构指向交替磁序参量，而非普通 Zeeman 效应。

    为什么 g-wave 有四个节点面？

    “g-wave”对应角动量量子数 ℓ = 4 的角向结构，较 p/d/f-wave 更高阶。CrSb 中的序参量近似 r⁴ sin³θ cosθ sin3φ。其中 cosθ = 0 给出 θ = 90° 节点；sin3φ = 0 给出每 60° 出现一次的方位角节点。结合晶体对称性后，实验有效识别为三个独立竖直节点面和一个水平节点面。

    为什么有效质量比例能证明两个频率来自同一个母费米面？

    若两个峰来自两个完全不同能带，它们的有效质量与频率无必然简单关系。若它们是同一费米面被交替磁劈裂出的自旋上、下“子面”，两者几何形状和费米速度轮廓相近，只是截面积略有差别，因此 m*/√f 应近似相同。实验中 m₁*/m₂* = 0.94 ± 0.04 与 √(f₁/f₂) = 0.945 一致，强烈支持“同一母面自旋劈裂”的解释。

    ---

    第五部分：创新评估与关联（Synthesis & Novelty）

    1. 创新性判断

    从表面证据推进到体相三维证据

    过去 2–5 年中，交替磁体研究大量依赖 ARPES、磁光、输运或理论预测。MnTe 的实验进展已显示 Kramers 简并解除，RuO₂、KV₂Se₂O 等体系则暴露出表面态、表面终止层与体相磁性不一致的问题。CrSb 的这项研究直接使用体敏感量子振荡，规避了表面 Rashba 态或表面交替磁性带来的误判风险。

    首次以量子振荡三维映射 g-wave 交替磁序参量

    此前工作多聚焦能带是否自旋劈裂，较少直接解析完整的序参量角向对称性。该研究不仅证明 CrSb 有自旋劈裂，还精确确定节点结构和实球谐函数形式 𝒴₄⁻³，把交替磁体从“是否存在”的判据推进到“序参量是什么”的层次。

    将非常规超导的节点序参量思想转化为金属磁体诊断框架

    非常规超导中直接测定节点能隙长期困难；这里通过 QO 频率是否分裂、何处坍缩，建立了非常规磁体序参量的可操作实验判据。这个思想创新在于把 p/d/f/g-wave 节点分类和费米面自旋劈裂统一起来。

    解决 CrSb 体相交替磁性确认问题

    CrSb 此前已有表面敏感光电子实验提示 g-wave 大劈裂，但体相证据不足。该研究通过 0.4 K 高分辨 QO、三类旋转几何、DFT 对照、有效质量比例和约 25 meV 劈裂估计，构成相互支撑的证据链，确认 CrSb 是体相金属交替磁体。

    提出适用于其他金属交替磁候选物的通用方法

    核心方法不依赖 CrSb 特有表面条件，而依赖金属体系的高质量晶体和可观测量子振荡。只要候选材料具有清晰费米面与足够长准粒子寿命，同样可以通过角分辨 QO 判断其节点面、反节点面和序参量不可约表示。CrSb 因残余电阻率低至 2 μΩ cm、磁有序温度约 740 K、空气稳定且元素易得，成为后续自旋电子学和非常规磁性研究的标杆平台。

    阅读原文 ↗
    原文链接页内定位
  13. 13
    Keffer 式对称海森堡交换及其自旋波影响#
    Keffer-like form of the symmetric Heisenberg exchange integral: Contribution to the Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert equation and spin wave dispersion dependence#
    📊 含图深析
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Pavel A. Andreev🔥 AI 1

    📄 摘要：讨论含 ligand 位移的 Keffer 式对称海森堡交换积分，指出其除影响 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用外，也可能修正对称交换常数，从而进入 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程并改变自旋波色散。

    💡 亮点：把键中心偏移对称地引入海森堡交换常数，进而改写 LLG 和自旋波色散。对精细建模磁超交换很有用。

    📖 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题非磁配体位移是否只能通过 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya 反对称交换影响多铁磁性？论文追问：配体几何偏移能否进入对称 Heisenberg 标量交换积分，并在反铁磁材料中产生新的自旋力矩、极化机制和自旋波色散特征。
    创新方法提出 Keffer-like 对称交换奇各向异性 OASEI：把交换积分写成 Uᵢⱼ = U₀,ᵢⱼ + U₁,ᵢⱼ(rᵢⱼ·δ₃) + U₂,ᵢⱼ(rᵢⱼ·[δ₂×δ₁])。Aha 点是：配体位移不再只生成 DMI 向量，而是进入对称 Heisenberg 交换常数；由于 rᵢⱼ 与 A/B 子晶格配体位移同时反号，交换积分仍保持 Uᵢⱼ = Uⱼᵢ，却在连续极限中产生一阶空间导数项。
    工作流程从反铁磁 A/B 子晶格与偏移配体几何出发，构造含 δ₃ 或 [δ₁×δ₂] 的有效位移 δₑff；将其写入标量 Heisenberg Hamiltonian；用量子流体动力学方法计算 Hamiltonian 对自旋算符的对易子；推导 LLG 中的方向导数力矩、Euler 方程力场、宏观能量密度；再把力矩改写为有效自旋流散度，得到多铁电极化；最后与 XYZ 反铁磁模型、DMI 项结合，分析共线和 cycloidal 自旋序下的自旋波色散变化。
    关键结果得到新的 LLG 力矩：∂ₜS_A = −(1/3)gₗ[S_A×(δₑff·∇)S_B]，∂ₜS_B = +(1/3)gₗ[S_B×(δₑff·∇)S_A]；得到 Lifshitz-like 能量密度 ℰ = (1/12)gₗ[L·(δₑff·∇)M − M·(δₑff·∇)L]；得到无空间梯度的新极化 P = (1/3)(γ/c)gₗ[S_A(S_B·δₑff) − S_B(S_A·δₑff)]。自旋波方面，OASEI 不像 DMI 给出线性 k 偏移，而是在频率平方中产生 (δₑff·k)² 型修正，并混合 δL_z 与 δM_z 两个反铁磁模。
    应用价值为多铁材料中的未知极化分量、反铁磁自旋波模混合和复杂介电响应提供新的微观解释；给出区分 DMI 与对称交换奇各向异性的实验线索：DMI 表现为线性波矢非互易或色散极小值偏移，OASEI 表现为沿配体有效位移方向的二次色散修正和 L/M 模干涉。该理论可指导反铁磁多铁材料的第一性原理参数提取、非弹性中子散射、自旋波谱和电磁振子实验分析。

    第一部分：核心概览

    提出一种Keffer-like 的对称 Heisenberg 交换积分奇各向异性：配体位移不仅可进入 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya 相互作用，也可进入对称交换常数。该项在连续极限中产生含一阶空间导数的能量密度、Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert 自旋力矩、有效自旋流与新的多铁电极化结构，并改变反铁磁自旋波色散。其地位在于把通常归于反对称交换的配体几何效应扩展到对称交换通道。

    ---

    第二部分：结构化快速回顾

    《Keffer-like form of the symmetric Heisenberg exchange integral: Contribution to the Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert equation and spin wave dispersion dependence》（None，）

    背景

    多铁性材料中的磁电效应需要微观机制解释。已有机制包括spin-current model、Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction、p–d hybridization等。Keffer 形式中 DMI 常数满足 D ∼ r₁₂ × δ，说明非磁配体位移可决定自旋相互作用的几何结构。

    方法

    从含标量交换积分的 Heisenberg Hamiltonian 出发，将交换积分推广为

    Uᵢⱼ = U₀,ᵢⱼ + U₁,ᵢⱼ(rᵢⱼ·δ₃) + U₂,ᵢⱼ(rᵢⱼ·[δ₂×δ₁])。

    使用多粒子量子流体动力学方法推导自旋力矩、Euler 动量方程中的力场、宏观能量密度与有效自旋流。

    结果

    新相互作用产生 LLG 方程中的一阶导数力矩：

    ∂ₜS_A = −(1/3)g₍ₗ₎[S_A × (δₑff·∇)S_B]，

    ∂ₜS_B = +(1/3)g₍ₗ₎[S_B × (δₑff·∇)S_A]。

    能量密度为

    ℰ = (1/12)g₍ₗ₎[L·(δₑff·∇)M − M·(δₑff·∇)L]。

    极化为

    P = (1/3)(γ/c)g₍ₗ₎[S_A(S_B·δₑff) − S_B(S_A·δₑff)]。

    讨论

    该相互作用只适用于反铁磁或亚铁磁材料，因其需要 A/B 子晶格配体位移的反号性质。其结构类似 DMI 的 Keffer 几何起源，但属于对称 Heisenberg 通道，并能在 spin-current model 中给出新的极化来源。

    局限

    推导主要为理论构造；未给出具体材料的第一性原理数值、实验拟合参数或统计检验。系数 U₁, U₂, g₍ₗ₎ 需要由非共线平衡态、实验色散或材料计算估计。

    结论

    配体位移可导致对称交换积分的奇各向异性 OASEI，从而引入一阶导数能量项、新自旋力矩、新有效自旋流与新多铁电极化机制，并与 DMI、XYZ 模型和反铁磁自旋波动力学发生耦合。

    ---

    第三部分：逐章节冷凝解析

    Abstract

    Keffer geometry is extended from antisymmetric to symmetric exchange

    核心命题是：symmetric Heisenberg exchange interaction 与 antisymmetric Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction 都可视为由磁离子经非磁离子超交换形成的有效自旋—自旋张量势的一部分。原文明确给出背景：*"The symmetric Heisenberg exchange interaction and antisymmetric Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction are parts of the tensor potential describing effective spin-spin interaction caused by the superexchange interaction of magnetic ions via nonmagnetic ion."* 关键创新是把 ligand shift 引入对称交换常数，而不仅限于 DMI 的 Keffer 向量常数。

    The Heisenberg constant is decomposed into at least two contributions

    交换常数至少含一个常规项与一个配体位移相关项。常规项产生无导数或二阶导数的标准能量密度；新项产生含一阶空间导数的能量密度。原文直接表述为：*"the constant of the Heisenberg interaction is composed minimum of two terms"*，并进一步说明：*"additional term gives a term in the energy density containing one spatial derivative of the spin density."*

    New torque and modified spin-wave dispersions follow from the odd anisotropy

    新相互作用给出 LLG 方程中的新贡献，并可能支持非共线平衡自旋序。原文强调：*"Corresponding contribution in the Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert equation is found"*，以及 *"Possibility of the noncollinear equilibrium order of spin under influence of new spin torque is discussed."* 对反铁磁体系，分别考虑共线序与cycloidal order下的自旋波正常模色散修正：*"Modification of the spin wave (normal modes) dispersion dependencies in the antiferromagnetic materials is found for the collinear order and for the cycloidal order of spins."*

    Effective spin current links the interaction to multiferroic polarization

    新相互作用不仅改变磁动力学，也通过 spin-current model 进入电极化机制。摘要明确给出：*"Effective spin current is derived and applied for the spin-current model of the polarization origin in multiferroics."*

    ---

    I Introduction

    Magnetoelectric mechanisms motivate the microscopic extension

    21 世纪以来，多铁性材料因较高相变温度与潜在应用推动磁电效应研究。原文开篇指出：*"Beginning of the XXI century demonstrated the growth of the interest to the magnetoelectric phenomenon in the relation to the appearance of the multiferroic materials with the relatively large temperature of the phase transition."* 理论背景覆盖 Fiebig 2005、Nagaosa 2008、Tokura 2014、Dong 2015、Mostovoy 2024 等综述。

    Spin-current model serves as the conceptual bridge

    Katsura–Nagaosa–Balatsky 模型中，电偶极矩正比于自旋叉乘，即 dᵢⱼ ∼ [rᵢⱼ × [Sᵢ × Sⱼ]]。原文概括为：*"the electric dipole moment is proportional to the vector product of spins."* 该模型最初解释“non-collinear spins”极化机制，之后被扩展到至少两种极化机制：*"it can be applied at least to two regimes."*

    Symmetric exchange can generate spin current relevant to polarization

    Heisenberg 对称交换相关的 magnon spin current 可进入 spin-current model。原文给出关键逻辑：*"The magnon spin current associated with the Heisenberg symmetric exchange interaction can be applied in the spin-current model to describe this mechanism."* 其推论是：若若干相互作用共同形成非共线自旋序，对称交换可进一步负责电极化形成。

    DMI with Keffer constants explains scalar-product-type polarization in previous work

    已有扩展表明，若使用 Keffer 形式的 DMI 微观常数，电偶极矩正比于自旋标量积也可纳入 spin-current model。原文写道：*"the electric dipole moment proportional to the scalar product of spins can be described within the spin-current model"*，条件是使用 *"the spin-current of magnons related to the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction with the microscopic Dzyaloshinskii constants chosen in the Keffer form."*

    The main target is odd anisotropy of symmetric exchange interaction

    核心目标被明确定义为 odd anisotropy of the symmetric exchange interaction, OASEI。原文直接给出：*"Main goal of this paper is the suggestion of novel form of the spin-spin interaction related to the odd anisotropy of the symmetric exchange interaction (OASEI)."* 这种奇形式来自交换积分对磁离子间距投影的依赖：*"Presented odd form of the anisotropy is related to the dependence of the exchange integral on the projection of the interparticle distance between magnetic ions."*

    Keffer-like inspiration comes from D ∼ r₁₂ × δ

    DMI 的 Keffer 形式写作 D ∼ r₁₂ × δ，其中 δ 为配体偏离磁离子连线或局域电荷质心的位置，r₁₂ 为磁离子间矢量。原文引用：*"this form symmetric exchange integral is inspired by the Keffer form of the Dzyaloshinskii constant D, where D ∼ r12 × δ."*

    OASEI is restricted to antiferromagnetic materials

    DMI 常数有四类形式；其中两类可存在于铁磁和反铁磁材料，两类仅存在于反铁磁材料。新提出的 OASEI 仅适用于反铁磁材料。原文非常明确：*"Suggested in this paper the odd anisotropy of the symmetric exchange interaction can exist in antiferromagnetic materials only."*

    OASEI and Keffer DMI both produce first-derivative continuum terms

    新相互作用与 Keffer DMI 在微观 Hamiltonian 中都与 r₁₂ 成正比，因此连续化后能量密度与自旋力矩含一阶空间导数。原文说明：*"Both the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction with the microscopic Dzyaloshinskii constants chosen in the Keffer form and the symmetric anisotropic exchange interaction with the odd anisotropy have microscopic Hamiltonian proportional to the interparticle distance of the magnetic ions r12."* 结果是：*"It leads to the energy density and spin-torque depending on the first derivative on the space coordinate."*

    Spin-wave consequences differ from DMI

    DMI 对色散的贡献依赖波矢投影，导致频率极小值偏离零波矢。OASEI 的贡献则进入特征频率平方，表现为波矢投影平方项。原文对比为：*"The Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction gives contribution depending on the projection of the wave vector"*，而 *"the OASEI gives contribution in the dispersion dependence proportional to the square of characteristic frequency, so it gives contribution proportional to the square of the projection of the wave vector."*

    OASEI complicates dielectric response by mixing δL_z and δM_z

    反铁磁材料中，通常两条色散分支可分别视为 δL_z 与 δM_z 动力学；OASEI 会导致二者干涉。原文指出：*"The OASEI leads to interference of δLz and δMz. Hence, it leads to more complex dielectric response of the multiferroics."*

    ---

    II Keffer-like form of the exchange integral in symmetric Heisenberg Hamiltonian

    The scalar Heisenberg coefficient is generalized while preserving Uᵢⱼ = Uⱼᵢ

    起点是标准标量交换 Hamiltonian：

    Ĥ = −(1/2)∑ᵢ,ⱼ,ⱼ≠ᵢ Uᵢⱼ(Ŝᵢ·Ŝⱼ)。

    原文写作：*"We start our analysis with the presentation of Heisenberg Hamiltonian with the scalar coefficient."* 基本约束是交换积分对指标交换保持对称：*"Obviously this construction includes the requirement of the symmetry of the coefficient Uij = Uji."*

    The central exchange integral contains conventional and odd-anisotropic parts

    对反铁磁多铁材料，交换积分被写成

    Uᵢⱼ = U₀,ᵢⱼ + U₁,ᵢⱼ(rᵢⱼ·δ₃,ᵢⱼ−AB) + U₂,ᵢⱼ(rᵢⱼ·[δ₂,ᵢⱼ−AB × δ₁])。

    原文给出定义：*"We suggest the following structure for the scalar coefficient (exchange integral) for the antiferromagnetic multiferroic materials"*，并说明 U₀,ᵢⱼ 是普通交换积分：*"where U0,ij is the usual exchange integral."*

    Odd anisotropy is encoded by partial ligand shifts with antisymmetric sublattice behavior

    三类部分配体位移满足：δ₁ 为常量，δ₂,ji−BA = −δ₂,ij−AB，δ₃,ji−BA = −δ₃,ij−AB。原文给出：*"They includes the partial ligand shifts δ1, δ2,ij-AB, and δ3,ij-AB which have the following properties: δ1 is a constant, δ2,ji-BA = −δ2,ij-AB, and δ3,ji-BA = −δ3,ij-AB."* 这些反号性质确保 Uᵢⱼ = Uⱼᵢ：*"Described properties allows to ensure the fundamental property of symmetry of the exchange integral."*

    Equation (2) is explicitly declared the conceptual core

    该交换积分形式是整篇工作的主要概念基础。原文直接写道：*"Equation (2) is the major concept of this paper."* 后续全部宏观模型、自旋力矩、能量密度、极化与集体激发都由该式推导。

    The proposed anisotropy differs from conventional symmetric anisotropic exchange tensors

    传统对称各向异性交换通常写成 JᵢⱼαβSᵢ^αSⱼ^β，被称为 symmetric anisotropic exchange，且常被视为小于 on-site anisotropy。原文指出：*"The anisotropy of exchange interaction as the tensor structure of generalized exchange integral Jijαβ Siα Sjβ is considered... where authors refer to it as the symmetric anisotropic exchange."* 新机制不同之处在于交换积分作为磁离子间距函数具有奇依赖：*"we consider the anisotropy related to the functional dependence of the exchange integral as a function of the interparticle distance."*

    ---

    II.1 Heisenberg constant with combination of two ligand shifts

    Two-shift mechanism parallels one Keffer-type DMI geometry

    第一种 OASEI 对应材料中存在 DMI 能量密度

    ℰ = −2g₍₀ζ₁)δ₂,AB[S_A × S_B]。

    原文指出：*"First, we consider the odd anisotropy of the symmetric exchange, which can appear in the materials, where the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction exists in form of energy density ℰ = −2g(0ζ1)δ2,AB[SA × SB]."* 这说明该机制与特定 DMI 几何构型共享配体位移配置。

    The exchange integral uses δ₁ and δ₂ through a vector product

    该项显式包含两个部分配体位移：

    J₂,ᵢⱼ = U₂,ᵢⱼ(rᵢⱼ·[δ₁ × δ₂,ᵢⱼ−AB])。

    原文说明：*"This exchange integral explicitly contains two partial ligand shifts δ1 and δ2,AB."*

    Nonzero contribution requires [δ₁ × δ₂] parallel to rᵢⱼ

    几何条件是 [δ₁ × δ₂,ᵢⱼ−AB] 平行于磁离子相对距离 rᵢⱼ。原文写道：*"The vector product of the partial ligand shifts [δ1 × δ2,ij-AB] is parallel to the relative distance of the magnetic ions rij."* 因此内积非零：*"It gives possible nonzero additional part of the exchange integral J2,ij."*

    Exchange symmetry remains intact

    该机制仍满足 J₂,ᵢⱼ = J₂,ⱼᵢ。原文给出：*"We also obtain J2,ij = J2,ji as it is described above."*

    ---

    II.2 Heisenberg constant with the ligand shift parallel to the relative distance

    Single-shift mechanism uses δ₃ parallel to the magnetic-ion bond

    第二种 OASEI 由单一部分配体位移 δ₃,ᵢⱼ−AB 给出：

    J₁,ᵢⱼ = U₁,ᵢⱼ(rᵢⱼ·δ₃,ᵢⱼ−AB)。

    原文定义为：*"Another form of the odd anisotropy of the symmetric exchange can be related to the single partial ligand shift δ3,ij-AB."*

    The geometry requires δ₃ in addition to δ₁

    图 2 展示 δ₃ 与 δ₁ 同时存在，其中 δ₃ 平行于 r_AB。原文说明：*"Required partial shift δ3,ij-AB exists in addition to δ1, both of them are demonstrated in Fig. (2)."*

    ---

    II.3 Combined contribution of the ligand shifts

    Both microscopic mechanisms are unified by an effective ligand shift

    两类机制可统一写为

    Uᵢⱼ = lᵢⱼ δₑff,ᵢⱼ−AB^β rᵢⱼ^β。

    原文写道：*"We can present the potential under consideration in a form including both mechanisms."*

    δₑff represents either δ₃ or [δ₁ × δ₂]

    有效位移定义为 δₑff = δ₃ 或 δₑff = [δ₁ × δ₂]；耦合函数 lᵢⱼ 对应 U₁,ᵢⱼ 或 U₂,ᵢⱼ。原文表述：*"δeff,ij-AB = δ3,ij-AB or δeff,ij-AB = [δ1 × δ2,ij-AB], correspondingly."* 该统一形式为后续宏观推导提供紧凑符号。

    ---

    III Spin torque

    Quantum hydrodynamic method gives the torque directly from the Hamiltonian commutator

    自旋演化由多体波函数与 Hamiltonian 对自旋算符的对易子给出：

    ∂ₜS_A = (i/ℏ)∫dR∑ᵢ∈Aδ(r−rᵢ)Ψ†[Ĥ,Ŝᵢ]Ψ。

    原文写道：*"We apply the quantum hydrodynamic method for the derivation of the spin evolution equation."*

    The OASEI torque for sublattice A contains a directional derivative along δₑff

    A 子晶格自旋力矩为

    ∂ₜS_A = −(1/3)g₍ₗ₎[S_A × (δₑff·∇)S_B]，

    其中 g₍ₗ₎ ≡ ∫r²l(r)d³r。原文给出：*"Straightforward calculations lead to the following partial contribution of the suggested interaction in the Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert equation for subspecies A."*

    The B-sublattice torque has the opposite sign structure

    B 子晶格为

    ∂ₜS_B = +(1/3)g₍ₗ₎[S_B × (δₑff·∇)S_A]。

    原文写作：*"and, similarly for subspecies B."* 这种符号差异与 A/B 子晶格反铁磁结构及配体位移反号性质一致。

    In L and M variables the torque couples staggered and net magnetization channels

    定义 L = S_A − S_B、M = S_A + S_B 后，新相互作用给出

    ∂ₜL = (1/6)g₍ₗ₎[M×(δₑff·∇)M] − [L×(δₑff·∇)L]，

    ∂ₜM = (1/6)g₍ₗ₎[L×(δₑff·∇)M] − [M×(δₑff·∇)L]。

    原文说明：*"It also allows to make transition to the antiferromagnetic vectors L... and M..."*

    需要注意，原始排版中写成 L = S_A − S_A、M = S_A + S_A，结合全文上下文应为 S_A − S_B 与 S_A + S_B。

    ---

    IV Force field in the Euler momentum balance equation

    The force field is derived because Euler momentum balance underlies the spin-current model

    Euler 方程中的力场有助于理解自旋与极化的静态和动态性质。原文指出：*"The Euler equation plays an important role in the derivation of the spin-current model."* 动量密度定义为 m j_A = mn v_A，其中 m 为离子质量、n 为浓度、v 为速度场。

    Zeroth-order expansion vanishes after angular integration and integration by parts

    零阶展开出现相互作用常数

    ∫(δαβl(r)+(r^αr^β/r)dl/dr)d³r，角平均后为

    δαβ∫(l(r)+(r/3)dl/dr)d³r。

    原文给出消失结论：*"Further integration by part in the second term shows that all interaction constant is equal to zero."*

    The first nonzero force field appears at second order in relative distance

    A 子晶格力场为

    F_A = m∂ₜj_A = −(1/3)g₍ₗ₎S_A^γ(δₑff·∇)∇S_B^γ。

    原文写道：*"Nonzero value of the force field appears in the second order expansion on the small relative distance."*

    The B-sublattice force has the opposite sign

    B 子晶格力场为

    F_B = m∂ₜj_B = +(1/3)g₍ₗ₎S_B^γ(δₑff·∇)∇S_A^γ。

    原文说明：*"Similarly, we derive the force field for the second subspecies B."*

    ---

    V Energy density

    The energy density is derived for comparison with standard magnetic models

    尽管自旋方程与力场已由 Hamiltonian 直接获得，显式能量密度仍有必要，因为磁性材料研究通常以能量密度表述相互作用。原文写道：*"we need explicit form of the energy density for comprehensive comparison with other known contributions in the model of the magnetically ordered materials."*

    The microscopic definition uses the A-sublattice local energy density

    A 子晶格能量密度定义为

    ℰ_A = −(1/2)∫dR∑ᵢ∈A,ⱼ∈Bδ(r−rᵢ)Ψ†Uᵢⱼ(Ŝᵢ·Ŝⱼ)Ψ。

    原文引入为：*"we present the definition of the macroscopic energy density in terms of the microscopic parameters."*

    The first nonzero continuum term appears at first order in relative distance

    与力场不同，能量密度的一阶相对距离展开即非零。原文明确：*"Non-zero value of the energy density appears in the first order expansion on the relative distance."*

    Partial energy densities have opposite signs for A and B

    A 子晶格：

    ℰ_A = (1/6)g₍ₗ₎[S_A·(δₑff·∇)S_B]。

    B 子晶格：

    ℰ_B = −(1/6)g₍ₗ₎[S_B·(δₑff·∇)S_A]。

    原文分别写道：*"we find the partial energy density for species 'A'"* 与 *"Similarly, we obtain the partial energy density for species 'B'."*

    The total energy density has a Lifshitz-invariant-like L–M structure

    合并后

    ℰ = (1/12)g₍ₗ₎L·(δₑff·∇)M − M·(δₑff·∇)L。

    原文给出：*"These partial energy densities can be combined in the full energy density... represented in terms of the antiferromagnetic vectors L... and M."*

    该形式类似 Lifshitz invariant：两个序参量之间的一阶导数反对称组合，但其来源是对称 Heisenberg 交换积分的奇各向异性，而非传统 DMI。

    ---

    VI Spin-current model

    Spin-current model connects antisymmetric spin current to electric polarization

    Katsura 模型中，非共线自旋导致电偶极矩 dᵢⱼ ∼ [rᵢⱼ × [Sᵢ × Sⱼ]]，宏观上对应 P^μ ∼ εμαβJαβ。原文说明：*"Relation between the electric polarization of spin origin and the spin current is point out in Ref. Katsura PRL 05."*

    Quantum hydrodynamic derivations broaden the spin-current model

    后续工作表明 spin-current model 可由量子流体动力学推导，不只是一种现象学假设。原文写道：*"the spin current model can be derived purely in terms of the quantum hydrodynamic model."*

    Different interactions generate different polarization regimes through different spin currents

    Heisenberg 交换的有效 magnon spin current 给出“non-collinear spins”极化；Keffer DMI 相关 spin current 给出“collinear spins”极化。原文对比：*"the effective spin current of magnons... gives the polarization for the regime of 'non-collinear spins'"*，而 *"the part of spin current of magnons described by the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction... gives the polarization for regime of 'collinear spins'."*

    Not all torques generate polarization

    只有能写成有效自旋流散度且含反对称部分的力矩才能产生极化。原文强调：*"the torques caused by some interactions cannot be presented as the divergence of the effective spin current or this spin current tensor can be symmetric, so they do not give any contribution in the polarization formation."*

    ---

    VI.1 Contribution of novel interaction in the polarization formation via the spin-current model

    The complete effective spin current follows from extracting a divergence from the total torque

    总自旋流为

    J_Σαβ = J_Aαβ+J_Bαβ = −(1/3)εαμνg₍ₗₑff)δₑff^β S_A^μS_B^ν。

    原文说明：*"It can be considered as the result of the extraction of the divergence from the complete spin torque Tα: Tα = ∂βJΣαβ."*

    The resulting polarization has no spatial derivatives

    极化表达式为

    P = (1/3)(γ/c)g₍ₗ₎[S_A(S_B·δₑff) − S_B(S_A·δₑff)]。

    原文指出其与 Mostovoy 形式有相似性但无空间导数：*"which shows some similarity to the structure suggested in Mostovoy PRL 06, but here we have no space derivatives of the spin density."*

    The polarization measures deviation of spin projections along the effective ligand direction

    (S_A·δₑff) 与 (S_B·δₑff) 是局域自旋在有效配体方向上的投影。组合

    S_A(S_B·δₑff) − S_B(S_A·δₑff)

    表示固定位置处两个子晶格自旋投影的偏差。原文解释：*"we can call (SA · δeff) as the local spin projection on the effective ligand direction."*

    The microscopic electric dipole moment has a triple-product structure

    与宏观极化对应的微观电偶极矩为

    d̂ᵢⱼ = (1/6)(γ/c)rᵢⱼ²l(rᵢⱼ)[δₑff,ᵢⱼ × [Ŝᵢ × Ŝⱼ]]。

    原文引入为：*"we need to present the electric dipole moment leading to the macroscopic polarization (20)."*

    The mechanism is only distantly similar to p–d hybridization

    该极化与 spin-dependent p–d hybridization 有结构上的远似，但 Tokura 综述中相关项含磁离子相对距离，宏观化后产生空间导数。原文写道：*"Polarization (20) shows some distant similarity with the spin dependent p–d hybridization mechanism."*

    ---

    VI.2 Comparison with known form of polarization

    Known continuum polarization contains spin-density gradients

    经典形式为

    P ∼ (S·∇)S − S(∇·S)。

    原文列出：*"Equation (20) following from the suggested interaction has some similarity in structure with polarization P ∼ (S · ∇)S − S(∇ · S)."*

    The standard gradient form originates from symmetric isotropic exchange spin current

    该形式可由对称各向同性 Heisenberg 交换的 magnon spin current 得到。原文说明：*"polarization (22) can be found using the spin current of magnons given by the symmetric isotropic exchange interaction."*

    The new polarization originates from symmetric anisotropic exchange instead

    新式 P = (1/3)(γ/c)g₍ₗ₎[S_A(S_B·δₑff) − S_B(S_A·δₑff)] 来源于对称各向异性交换。原文明确区分：*"While polarization (20) follows from the symmetric anisotropic exchange interaction."*

    ---

    VII Energy density and spin torque for antiferromagnetic materials in XYZ model

    The XYZ model supplies the baseline interactions for spin-wave analysis

    后续集体激发计算需要同时包含常规交换、各向异性和新 OASEI。原文说明：*"Before we consider the collective excitations following the model developed above we describe other interactions included in our calculations."*

    The microscopic Hamiltonian separates isotropic exchange and z-axis anisotropy

    XYZ Hamiltonian 为

    Ĥ = −(1/2)∑ₙ∈A,ₖ∈B(UₙₖxxŜₙ^xŜₖ^x + UₙₖyyŜₙ^yŜₖ^y + UₙₖzzŜₙ^zŜₖ^z)，

    并重写为

    −(1/2)∑(Uₙₖ(Ŝₙ·Ŝₖ₎₊κ̃ₙₖŜₙ^zŜₖ^z)。

    原文给出：*"Mainly we consider the XYZ model for uniaxial crystal."*

    The model assumes Uxx=Uyy=U and Uzz=U+κ̃

    横向交换等同，纵向包含额外各向异性：Uₙₖxx=Uₙₖyy=Uₙₖ，Uₙₖzz=Uₙₖ+κ̃ₙₖ。原文写作：*"where Unk = Unkxx = Unkyy, and Unkzz = Unk + κ̃nk."*

    ---

    VII.1 Energy density in XYZ model

    The full continuum energy contains local and Laplacian exchange-anisotropy terms

    A 子晶格能量密度包含 AA 与 AB 相互作用、各向同性项与 z 方向各向异性项，以及二阶梯度项：

    g₀,u,AA(S_A·S_A)、κ₀,AA(S_A^zS_A^z)、g₂,u,AA(S_A·△S_A)、κ₂,AA(S_A^z△S_A^z)，并有对应 AB 项。原文给出：*"After required simplification we obtain the energy density associated with one subspecies A."*

    B-subsystem energy follows by A ↔ B exchange

    原文写作：*"Expression for the second subspecies can be found from (24) as ℰB = ℰA(A ↔ B)."* 总能量密度为 ℰ = ℰ_A + ℰ_B。

    Under U_AB = −U_AA = −U_BB ≡ −U, only L appears in the simplified energy

    常用假设为

    U_AB = −U_AA = −U_BB ≡ −U。

    原文明确：*"If we apply the common assumption for the relation between the exchange integrals between subspecies UAB = −UAA = −UBB ≡ −U."* 于是简化总能量只含 L：

    ℰ = −(1/2)g₀,u(L·L) −(1/2)κ₀(L^zL^z) −(1/12)g₂,u(L·△L) −(1/12)κ₂(L^z△L^z)。

    The exchange stiffness is recovered in familiar gradient form

    定义 A ≡ g₂,u/6 后，交换能

    Eₑₓ = −(1/2)A∫(L·△L)d³r = (1/2)A∫(∂^βL·∂^βL)d³r。

    原文说明：*"so this term can be presented in more familiar form."*

    ---

    VII.2 Spin torque in XYZ model

    Spin dynamics uses a more general exchange hierarchy than the simplified energy case

    能量密度简化采用 U_AB = −U_AA = −U_BB，但动力学进一步允许 U_AA = U_BB ≠ |U_AB|。原文指出：*"But further analysis we make for more general case of UAA = UBB ≠ |UAB|."*

    Two exchange combinations U₊ and U₋ organize the torque coefficients

    定义

    U₊ = (U_AA + U_BB + 2|U_AB|)/4，

    U₋ = (U_AA + U_BB − 2|U_AB|)/4。

    原文说明：*"Described combined exchange integrals U+ and U− lead to two sets of the coefficients in the spin evolution equations."*

    The L equation contains exchange, anisotropy, Laplacian, and z-anisotropic Laplacian torques

    ∂ₜL 包含

    −g₀u[L×M]、κ₊[M×L_z e_z]、κ₋[L×M_z e_z]、A₊[M×△L]、A₋[L×△M]、(1/6)κ₂₊[M×△L_z e_z]、(1/6)κ₂₋[L×△M_z e_z]。

    原文完整列出方程，并说明后续会将其与 OASEI 力矩结合：*"We apply this set of symmetric Heisenberg Hamiltonian based the Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert equations in combination with the spin torque following from the suggested interaction."*

    The M equation describes anisotropy-driven and gradient-driven net magnetization dynamics

    ∂ₜM 包含

    κ₊[L×L_z e_z]、κ₋[M×M_z e_z]、A₊[L×△L]、A₋[M×△M]、(1/6)κ₂₊[L×△L_z e_z]、(1/6)κ₂₋[M×△M_z e_z]。

    该结构使新 OASEI 可与常规反铁磁模耦合，尤其影响 δL_z 与 δM_z 分支。

    ---

    VIII Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction

    Only a specific Keffer-like part of DMI is retained

    DMI 不作完整讨论，仅保留与配体位移 δ₂,ij−AB 成正比的一类 Dzyaloshinskii 常数：

    Dᵢⱼ,(δ) = ζ₁(rᵢⱼ₎δ₂,ij−AB。

    原文写道：*"we consider a part of the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction with the Dzyaloshinskii constant of the following form."*

    This DMI part yields a standard local cross-product energy density

    对应能量密度为

    ℰ = −2g₍₀ζ₁)δ₂,AB[S_A × S_B]。

    原文说明该形式为：*"which leads to the well-known form of the energy density."*

    它与 II.1 中两位移 OASEI 共享几何背景，但属于反对称交换通道。

    DMI is used as a comparator and possible coexistence interaction

    DMI 的作用是为 OASEI 提供参照：二者都可由配体位移几何产生，但 DMI 贡献通常线性依赖波矢投影并可移动色散极小值；OASEI 贡献进入特征频率平方并产生波矢投影平方型修正。Introduction 中相关原文为：*"The Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction gives contribution depending on the projection of the wave vector"*，而 OASEI 为 *"contribution proportional to the square of the projection of the wave vector."*

    ---

    IX Contribution of the novel interaction in the dispersion dependence of the spin waves

    原始正文缺失导致无法逐式还原 IX 节推导

    已给出的 Introduction 与 Abstract 确认 IX 节目标为计算 OASEI 对反铁磁自旋波正常模色散的贡献。原文目录句为：*"In Sec. IX contribution of the novel interaction in the dispersion dependence of the spin waves is calculated."*

    Collinear and cycloidal equilibrium orders are both included

    摘要明确给出 IX 节覆盖两类背景态：*"Modification of the spin wave (normal modes) dispersion dependencies in the antiferromagnetic materials is found for the collinear order and for the cycloidal order of spins."* 因此色散计算至少分为共线反铁磁序与旋摆/摆线 cycloidal 序两种情形。

    Expected qualitative dispersion effect is quadratic in wave-vector projection

    已提供 Introduction 给出关键定性结果：DMI 导致线性波矢投影项并使最小频率偏离零波矢；OASEI 进入频率平方并贡献 (δₑff·k)² 型修正。原文为：*"the OASEI gives contribution in the dispersion dependence proportional to the square of characteristic frequency, so it gives contribution proportional to the square of the projection of the wave vector."*

    OASEI mixes δL_z and δM_z branches

    普通反铁磁体系中两支色散可视为 δL_z 与 δM_z 独立动力学；OASEI 使二者耦合。原文为：*"Both the collinear equilibrium order and the cycloidal equilibrium order in antiferromagnetic materials shows the independence of two branches of the dispersion dependence"*，但随后指出：*"The OASEI leads to interference of δLz and δMz."*

    ---

    X Brief summary of obtained results

    原始正文缺失导致无法逐句解析 X 节

    目录显示 X 节为总结：*"In Sec. X a brief summary of obtained results is presented."*

    可确认的总结性结果包括四项

    第一，提出 symmetric Heisenberg exchange integral 的 Keffer-like 奇各向异性。第二，推导含 (δₑff·∇) 的 LLG 力矩。第三，给出能量密度 ℰ = (1/12)g₍ₗ₎[L·(δₑff·∇)M − M·(δₑff·∇)L]。第四，得到新 spin-current 极化

    P = (1/3)(γ/c)g₍ₗ₎[S_A(S_B·δₑff) − S_B(S_A·δₑff)]。这些均由已提供正文和摘要支持。

    ---

    第四部分：深度理解与语言支持

    1. 术语解析

    Odd anisotropy of the symmetric exchange interaction, OASEI

    “odd” 指交换积分对磁离子间距矢量投影 rᵢⱼ·δₑff 呈奇函数型依赖；当交换 i ↔ j 时，rᵢⱼ 与部分配体位移同时反号，使整体交换积分仍满足 Uᵢⱼ = Uⱼᵢ。它不同于普通偶函数型交换各向异性，如 J(|z|, √(x²+y²))，后者通常产生二阶空间导数项。

    Keffer form

    Keffer 形式通常指 DMI 常数的几何表达 D ∼ r₁₂ × δ，强调 DMI 由磁离子连线与配体偏移共同决定。这里的 “Keffer-like” 不是说对称交换常数也变成叉乘向量，而是说同样把配体位移几何纳入相互作用常数。

    Ligand shift / partial ligand shift

    ligand shift 是非磁离子，如氧离子，相对局域电荷中心或磁离子连线的偏移。文中拆成 δ₁、δ₂、δ₃ 三种“partial”位移，分别用于构造不同几何机制：δ₃ 平行磁离子连线；δ₂ 可与 δ₁ 叉乘形成平行于 rᵢⱼ 的有效矢量。

    Spin-current model

    这里不是电子输运意义上的真实电荷流，而是由自旋相互作用力矩重写为有效自旋流散度后得到的张量 Jαβ。其反对称部分可通过 P^μ = (γ/c)εμαβJαβ 产生电极化。

    Cycloidal order

    cycloidal order 是自旋在空间中沿某一传播方向周期性旋转，且旋转平面包含传播方向；常见于多铁材料。它与螺旋 helical order 的区别在于旋转平面和传播矢量的相对取向不同，因而可诱发不同方向的电极化。

    ---

    2. 疑难查证

    为什么对称 Heisenberg 交换能产生类似 DMI 的一阶导数项？

    通常 Heisenberg 交换 U(r)(Sᵢ·Sⱼ₎ 中，U(r) 是偶函数，连续展开时一阶项因空间反演或角平均抵消，保留的是 S·△S 或 (∇S)² 这类二阶导数项。这里交换积分含 rᵢⱼ·δₑff。由于 rᵢⱼ 本身是一阶小量，配合 S_B(r+rᵢⱼ) 的 Taylor 展开，一阶导数项不再被对称性消去，最终出现 S_A·(δₑff·∇)S_B。关键不是 Hamiltonian 的自旋部分反对称，而是交换积分的空间几何依赖具有“奇”结构。

    为什么这种 OASEI 只适用于反铁磁材料？

    若只有一种等价磁子晶格，交换 i ↔ j 时很难让奇的 rᵢⱼ 因子保持标量交换积分对称。反铁磁材料有 A/B 子晶格，配体位移可定义为 δₑff,ji−BA = −δₑff,ij−AB。于是 rⱼᵢ = −rᵢⱼ 与 δₑff,ji−BA = −δₑff,ij−AB 同时反号，内积 r·δₑff 保持不变，从而满足 Uᵢⱼ = Uⱼᵢ。这就是“奇各向异性”与交换积分对称性并存的数学条件。

    为什么新极化式没有空间导数，却仍来自含一阶导数的力矩？

    力矩可写成 T^α = ∂_βJαβ。一阶导数出现在力矩中，是因为对有效自旋流 Jαβ 求散度；但自旋流本身可以只含局域自旋乘积与 δₑff，即

    J_Σαβ = −(1/3)εαμνg₍ₗ₎δₑff^βS_A^μS_B^ν。

    代入极化关系 P^μ = (γ/c)εμαβJαβ 后，空间导数已被“积分回”自旋流结构中，因此极化是局域形式。

    δL_z 与 δM_z 的“interference”意味着什么？

    反铁磁小振幅动力学常可分解为 staggered magnetization L 与 net magnetization M 的两个独立模，尤其 z 方向扰动 δL_z 和 δM_z 可形成两条独立色散分支。OASEI 的能量密度含 L·(δₑff·∇)M − M·(δₑff·∇)L，天然把 L 与 M 的空间变化交叉耦合。在线性化方程中，这会产生非对角项，使原本独立的 δL_z 与 δM_z 模混合，从而改变介电响应与电磁振子谱线。

    ---

    第五部分：创新评估与关联

    1. 创新性判断

    新颖性一：把 Keffer 配体位移机制从 DMI 推广到对称 Heisenberg 交换

    近年关于多铁电极化、DMI、spin-current model 的工作多集中于反对称交换或传统对称交换自旋流。该工作提出：配体位移不仅决定 Dzyaloshinskii vector，也可进入标量 Heisenberg exchange integral，形成 Uᵢⱼ = U₀ + U₁(r·δ₃)+U₂(r·[δ₂×δ₁])。这解决了此前模型中“配体几何效应主要归属 DMI”的局限。

    新颖性二：提出对称交换的一阶导数 Lifshitz-like 能量项

    传统对称交换在连续极限中通常给出 无导数项或二阶导数/交换刚度项；一阶导数项通常与 DMI 或 Lifshitz invariant 相关。这里从对称 Heisenberg Hamiltonian 的奇各向异性交换积分推得

    ℰ = (1/12)g₍ₗ₎[L·(δₑff·∇)M − M·(δₑff·∇)L]，为一阶导数项提供了新的微观来源。

    新颖性三：给出新的 LLG 自旋力矩通道

    新力矩

    ∂ₜS_A = −(1/3)g₍ₗ₎[S_A×(δₑff·∇)S_B]

    不同于常规交换力矩、DMI 力矩和单离子各向异性力矩。它能驱动或稳定非共线平衡结构，并在反铁磁动力学中混合 L 与 M 模。

    新颖性四：提出新的 spin-current 极化形式

    通过有效自旋流得到无梯度极化

    P = (1/3)(γ/c)g₍ₗ₎[S_A(S_B·δₑff) − S_B(S_A·δₑff)]。

    该形式不同于 Katsura 的叉乘型极化、Mostovoy/Baryakhtar 的梯度型极化，也不同于传统 p–d hybridization 的局域单自旋方向依赖机制。它为多铁材料中尚未归类的极化分量提供候选解释。

    新颖性五：预言不同于 DMI 的自旋波色散修正

    DMI 常导致线性 k 非互易项或频率极小值偏移；OASEI 对色散的主效应进入频率平方，并给出 (δₑff·k)² 型贡献，同时混合 δL_z 与 δM_z。这为实验上区分 DMI 与对称交换奇各向异性提供了可观测路径：观察色散是否表现为线性非互易偏移，还是二次投影修正与模混合。

    总体评价

    理论创新强，核心价值在于提出一个新的微观相互作用类别并系统推导其宏观后果。主要未解决部分是材料实现与参数量级：U₁、U₂、g₍ₗ₎、δₑff 尚需第一性原理计算、非弹性中子散射、自旋波谱或电磁振子实验验证。

    阅读原文 ↗
    原文链接页内定位
  14. 14
    方格晶格斯格明子相的电磁场耦合#
    Interplay of electric and magnetic fields in skyrmion phases of the classical Heisenberg model on a square lattice#
    📊 含图深析
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 A. Vela Wac, F. A. Gómez Albarracín, D. C. Cabra🔥 AI 1

    📄 摘要：在方格晶格经典海森堡磁电模型中，用蒙特卡罗模拟研究磁场与电场共同作用下的斯格明子相，分析不同场强、温度和耦合参数对拓扑自旋纹理稳定区间的影响，并刻画电场对其生成与调控的作用。

    💡 亮点：电场和磁场共同决定方格磁电体中的斯格明子稳定区。为可电控拓扑自旋纹理提供了参数图景。

    📖 展开分析 + 配图
    研究问题在平方晶格经典 Heisenberg–Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya 模型中，外加磁场 Bz 与不同方向电场如何共同重塑斯格明子相：电场能否压缩、拉伸、湮灭或生成 SkX、SkG、双半子等拓扑磁纹理，并改变手性相稳定窗口。
    创新方法将 d–p hybridization 局域磁电耦合写入经典自旋哈密顿量，用 Pi=λ(SySz,SzSx,SxSy) 把每个自旋直接映射为电偶极；同时扫描 B–E 二维相图，并把自旋纹理与电偶极/电四极纹理并排分析，揭示“斯格明子=电四极纹理载体”的可视化机制。
    工作流程输入平方晶格自旋系统、J=D=1、λ=0.2、磁场 Bz 与两类电场 E∥(1,1,0)、E∥(0,0,1)；通过 Metropolis 蒙特卡洛从高温降至 T≈10⁻³，并在 B–E 平面逐点采样；计算自旋纹理、偶极纹理、结构因子、磁化 M、极化 P、标量手性 χ；输出 FM、FE、Sp、SkX、SkG、Bm 等相区边界和相互转化路径。
    关键结果面内电场 E∥(1,1,0) 会压缩并破坏 SkX，约 E≈3 时显著削弱斯格明子晶格，同时把手性窗口推向更低 Bz，使低磁场下可出现 SkG；面外电场 E∥z 使斯格明子沿 xy 对角方向拉长，在高磁场原本铁磁区生成 SkG，并进一步诱导 bimeron-rich 相；相边界处磁化、极化和手性同步突变，结构因子从六峰 SkX 转为环状 SkG、单 q 螺旋或展宽双半子峰。
    应用价值为 II 型多铁材料中用电场低功耗操控拓扑磁态提供微观图像：可通过改变电场方向选择性实现斯格明子晶格压缩、拉伸、生成、湮灭和双半子转化；极化异常与磁化异常可作为实验识别 SkX、SkG、Bm 相变的信号，面向多铁自旋电子学和可调拓扑信息器件。

    第一部分：核心概览 (Overview)

    该研究通过经典蒙特卡洛模拟系统刻画平方晶格铁磁 Heisenberg–Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya 模型中，磁场 (B_z) 与两种方向电场 (mathbf Eparallel(1,1,0))、(mathbf Eparallel(0,0,1)) 对斯格明子相的协同调控。核心贡献在于给出完整低温 ((B,E)) 相图，揭示电场不仅能压缩、拉伸、湮灭斯格明子晶格，还能把螺旋相、斯格明子气、双半子富集相相互转化。研究把自旋纹理与由 d–p hybridization 诱导的电偶极/四极纹理并行分析，提供了理解 II 型多铁材料中电场控制拓扑磁态的微观框架。

    ---

    第二部分：结构化快速回顾

    《Interplay of electric and magnetic fields in skyrmion phases of the classical Heisenberg model on a square lattice》（None，）

    背景

    磁性斯格明子是由 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction, DMI 等机制稳定的拓扑自旋纹理，具备低功耗自旋电子学潜力。传统电流操控存在欧姆热限制；磁电材料尤其是 type-II multiferroics 中，电极化由磁序诱导，提供了电场低耗操控磁纹理的可能。

    方法

    采用平方晶格经典 Heisenberg 模型，包含铁磁交换 (J=1)、DMI (D=1)、磁场 (B_z)、电场 (mathbf E)，以及由 d–p hybridization 给出的局域极化

    [

    mathbf Pᵢ₌łambda(Sᵢ^ySᵢ^z,Sᵢ^zSᵢ^x,Sᵢ^xSᵢ^y),quad łambda=0.2.

    ]

    使用 Metropolis 蒙特卡洛，主系统 (L=48)，周期边界，降温至 (Tapprox10⁻³)，并用 (L=60,72) 检查有限尺寸效应。

    结果

    识别出 FM、FE、Sp、SkX、SkG、Bm 等相。面内电场 (mathbf Eparallel(1,1,0)) 倾向于压缩并破坏 SkX，但也能把手性区域推向更低 (B_z)。面外电场 (mathbf Eparallel z) 使斯格明子沿 (xy) 方向拉伸，并促进 bimeron-rich 相。磁化、极化和手性在相边界同步变化。

    讨论

    电场调控并非简单改变能量尺度，而是通过局域磁电耦合重塑斯格明子内部结构。斯格明子携带的电四极纹理使自旋拓扑与电偶极空间一一关联；因此，极化响应可作为多铁斯格明子相变的实验信号。

    局限

    模型为经典自旋、二维平方晶格、固定 (J=D=1)、固定 (łambda=0.2)，未纳入量子涨落、各向异性、真实材料晶格细节、长程偶极相互作用或实时动力学。相边界依赖 (D/J)，绝对数值不能直接等同于具体材料。

    结论

    交叉电磁场能系统性调控拓扑磁纹理：面内电场偏向破坏 SkX，面外电场偏向拉伸并诱导双半子；温度进一步产生 SkG、bimeron-rich 与手性无序中间态。自旋—电偶极双空间分析是理解多铁斯格明子相变的关键。

    ---

    第三部分：逐章节冷凝解析 (Section-by-Section Condensing)

    Abstract

    • Magnetoelectric control of skyrmion phases

    研究对象是带 DMI 和 磁电耦合 的平方晶格铁磁 Heisenberg 模型，目标是解析磁场与电场对斯格明子相的联合影响。摘要明确指出，磁性斯格明子因拓扑稳定性和低耗器件潜力而重要：*"Magnetic skyrmions are topologically stable spin textures that can be stabilized by Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction and manipulated by external fields, making them promising for low-dissipation spintronic applications."* 方法为经典蒙特卡洛：*"Here we investigate, using classical Monte Carlo simulations, the combined effects of magnetic and electric fields on skyrmion phases in a ferromagnetic Heisenberg model on the square lattice with Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction and magnetoelectric coupling via the d-p hybridization mechanism."*

    • Full phase taxonomy under crossed fields

    可辨识相包括 ferromagnetic, ferroelectric, spiral, skyrmion crystal, skyrmion gas, bimeron，并通过自旋纹理、偶极纹理、结构因子、磁化、极化、标量手性共同判定：*"We analyze spin and dipolar textures, structure factors, magnetization, polarization, and scalar chirality for different field orientations and strengths, identifying ferromagnetic, ferroelectric, spiral, skyrmion crystal, skyrmion gas, and bimeron phases, as well as the field-driven transitions between them."*

    • Electric-field reshaping of chiral stability windows

    电场显著改变手性相稳定区，不只是微扰变形；具体表现为斯格明子形变、转化为双半子、磁场手性窗口位移：*"We show that electric fields strongly reshape the stability region and internal structure of chiral phases, inducing skyrmion deformation, transmutation into bimerons, and shifts of the chiral window in magnetic field."*

    • Localized magnetoelectric entities as microscopic mediators

    局域磁电实体，尤其是带电四极纹理的斯格明子，是电场操控拓扑磁态的关键中介：*"Our results highlight the role of localized magnetoelectric entities, such as skyrmions carrying electric quadrupolar textures, in mediating electric-field control of topological magnetic states, providing a microscopic framework relevant to multiferroic skyrmion-host materials."*

    ---

    I Introduction

    • Historical and technological context of magnetic skyrmions

    斯格明子理论预言可追溯至上世纪，首个实验观测出现在 itinerant ferromagnet MnSi；其小尺寸和稳定性推动信息存储与处理研究。原文给出背景：*"Magnetic skyrmions were theoretically predicted in the past century [1, 2] and first observed in the itinerant ferromagnet MnSi [3, 4]."* 技术动机为：*"Due to their stability and small size, skyrmions are serious candidates for use in information storage and processing [12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18]."*

    • Multiple stabilization mechanisms beyond DMI

    典型稳定机制为 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction 加外磁场，但其他机制也可稳定斯格明子，包括磁挫折、各向异性耦合、RKKY、偶极相互作用。对应原文：*"These topologically nontrivial spin textures are typically stabilized by the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) [29, 30] in the presence of an external magnetic field."* 同时：*"alternative stabilization mechanisms have also been identified, including magnetic frustration [31, 32, 33], anisotropic couplings [34, 35, 36], Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) [37], and dipolar [38] interactions."*

    • Motivation for electric-field control in multiferroics

    金属体系中自旋转移力矩可用超低电流操控斯格明子，但欧姆热是主要限制；磁电材料提供低耗电场路径。原文指出：*"Skyrmions can be manipulated in metallic systems by ultralow current densities via spin-transfer torque [39, 40, 41, 42, 43, 44], although Ohmic heating remains a major limitation for spintronic applications."* 因此：*"This has motivated increasing interest in magnetoelectric (ME) materials, particularly type-II multiferroics, where the electric polarization is induced by the magnetic order itself."*

    • Three microscopic mechanisms of magnetoelectric coupling

    文中区分三类磁电耦合机制。第一，spin-current mechanism：

    [

    mathbf Pproptosumᵢⱼmathbf eᵢⱼ×(mathbf Sᵢ×mathbf Sⱼ₎

    ]

    原文：*"In noncollinear magnets, the spin-current mechanism generates a polarization of the form (ěcPproptosumᵢⱼěceᵢⱼ×(ěcSᵢ×ěcSⱼ))."*

    第二，symmetric exchange striction：(mathbf Sᵢḑotmathbf Sⱼ)，可在共线态诱导铁电性：*"symmetric exchange striction can induce ferroelectricity through terms proportional to (ěcSᵢḑotěcSⱼ), allowing polarization even in collinear states."*

    第三，局域 spin-dependent d–p hybridization：*"A third mechanism, particularly relevant in multiferroic insulators, is the spin-dependent d–p hybridization between transition-metal and ligand ions."*

    • Local d–p hybridization as the chosen mechanism

    与前两种依赖相邻自旋关联的机制不同，d–p hybridization 是局域机制，其偶极取决于单个自旋相对键方向的取向：

    [

    mathbf pᵢⱼpropto(mathbf eᵢⱼḑotmathbf Sᵢ₎²mathbf eᵢⱼ.

    ]

    原文强调局域性：*"Unlike the previous two, this mechanism is essentially local, with the induced dipole depending on the orientation of each spin relative to the bond direction."* 适用材料包括 CuFeO(₂)、CuCrO(₂)、Ba(₂)CoGe(₂)O(₇)、Cu(₂)OSeO(₃)。

    • Skyrmions carry electric dipolar or quadrupolar moments

    关键先验实验/理论动机来自 Cu(₂)OSeO(₃) 中斯格明子携带局域电多极矩。原文：*"Seki et al. [66] showed that skyrmions in Cu2OSeO3 carry localized electric dipolar or quadrupolar moments, enabling their manipulation by external electric fields."* 这些多极矩并非独立自由度，而由自旋纹理决定：*"These multipolar moments are not independent degrees of freedom, but are fully determined by the underlying spin texture through the magnetoelectric coupling."*

    • Novel systematic mapping of combined ((B,E)) phase diagrams

    相对于固定磁场或固定电场的既有工作，核心推进是系统绘制两种电场方向下的联合 ((B,E)) 相图。原文直接界定差异：*"In contrast to previous studies that focused on fixed magnetic or electric fields, here we provide a systematic mapping of the combined (B,E) phase diagrams for two distinct electric-field orientations."* 由此揭示电场不仅抑制或形变 SkX，还可诱导 Sk、SkG、Bm 相互转化：*"This approach reveals how electric fields not only suppress or deform skyrmion lattices, but also induce controlled transformations between skyrmion, skyrmion-gas, and bimeron-rich phases across extended regions of parameter space."*

    ---

    II Model and methods

    • Hamiltonian with exchange, DMI, magnetoelectric coupling, and Zeeman field

    模型为平方晶格上的经典 Heisenberg 自旋，哈密顿量为

    [

    mathcal H=-Jsumłₐₙgₗₑ ᵢ,ⱼåₙgₗₑmathbf Sᵢḑotmathbf Sⱼ

    +Dsumłₐₙgₗₑ ᵢ,ⱼåₙgₗₑδhatmathbf rḑot(mathbf Sᵢ×mathbf Sⱼ₎

    -mathbf Eḑotsumᵢmathbf Pᵢ

    -B_zsumᵢSᵢ^z.

    ]

    原文：*"We considered here a system composed of classical Heisenberg magnetic moments, in a square under uniform magnetic ((B_z)) and electric ((ěcE)) fields, given by the Hamiltonian."* 自旋为单位矢量：*"the spin variables are unimodular classical vectors, (|ěcSᵢ|=1)."* 近邻耦合与原胞矢量为：*"nearest neighbors (NN) coupling and the primitive translation vectors are (ěce₁=(1,0)) and (ěce₂=(0,1))."*

    • Effective polarization from spin-dependent metal–ligand hybridization

    局域极化由 d–p hybridization 约化为自旋分量二次型：

    [

    mathbf Pᵢ₌łambda(Sᵢ^ySᵢ^z,Sᵢ^zSᵢ^x,Sᵢ^xSᵢ^y).

    ]

    原文：*"we considered here the spin-dependent metal–ligand hybridization mechanism, which has been shown to describe the magnetoelectric response in chiral magnetic insulators such as Cu2OSeO3."* 具体公式来自对键方向平均与晶格对称性：*"Upon averaging over the bond directions (eᵢⱼ) and considering the symmetry of the lattice, this expression reduces to an effective quadratic form in the spin components."* 参数固定为 (łambda=0.2)：*"where (łambda) (set as 0.2 in this work) is the magnetoelectric coupling constant."*

    • Energy-scale convention and physical implication of the ME term

    设定 (J=1)，使 (E) 与 (łambda) 无量纲，磁电强度由 (łambdamathbf E) 控制：*"Setting (J=1) fixes the energy scale, so that both (ěcE) and (łambda) are dimensionless and the ME coupling is governed by (łambdaěcE)."* 电场偏好混合自旋分量，从而促进非共线纹理：*"the electric field favors configurations with mixed spin components, promoting noncollinear textures that can gain energy through the ME coupling."*

    • Monte Carlo protocol and statistical sampling

    采用 Metropolis MC，主模拟尺寸 (N=L²)、(L=48)，周期边界；降温率 (Tₙ₊₁=0.9Tₙ)，从 (T=2) 到 (Tapprox10⁻³)。原文给出精确采样量：*"At each temperature, up to (10⁵) Monte Carlo steps (MCS) were used for thermalization, followed by (2×10⁵) MCS for measurements."* 每组参数独立运行 10 次，相变附近 20 次：*"we performed 10 independent runs for each set of parameters, increasing this number to 20 in regions close to phase transitions."*

    • Hysteresis-like electric-field sweeps and finite-size checks

    低温固定 (Tapprox10⁻³) 下，从 (E=0) 扫到最大值再扫回，用于检验相稳定性：*"complementary simulations were carried out at fixed low temperature ((Tapprox10⁻³)), sweeping the electric field from (E=0) to a maximum value and back, in order to assess the stability of the observed phases."* 有限尺寸检查使用 (L=60,72)，每个选点 5 次独立模拟：*"we also performed 5 independent simulations for larger system sizes ((L=60) and (L=72)) at selected points of the phase diagram, confirming that the main features remain qualitatively unchanged."*

    • Structure factor as the ordering diagnostic

    横向结构因子

    [

    Sₚₑᵣₚ₍ₘₐₜₕbf q)=frac1Nłeftłangle

    łeft|sumⱼSⱼ^xeⁱmathbf qḑotmathbf rⱼi̊ght|²⁺

    łeft|sumⱼSⱼ^yeⁱmathbf qḑotmathbf rⱼi̊ght|²

    i̊ghtångle

    ]

    用于识别 Bragg 峰和相类型。原文：*"We have calculated the perpendicular (to z) component of the static spin structure factor in the reciprocal lattice ((ěcSₚₑᵣₚ(ěcq))) to identify the Bragg peaks that characterize the different spin-textures."*

    • Scalar chirality as discrete topological charge

    标量手性定义为

    [

    ḩi=łeftłanglefrac14πsumΔ_₁,Δ_₂

    mathbf Sᵢḑot(mathbf Sⱼ×mathbf Sₖ₎ᵢ̊ghtångle.

    ]

    平方晶格每个 plaquette 分解为两个三角形，采用一致逆时针顺序：*"On the square lattice, each plaquette is decomposed into two triangles, and a consistent counterclockwise ordering of the spins is adopted."* 在固定 (J,D,B_z>0) 符号下，斯格明子手性为正，电场不改变其符号：*"In the parameter regime explored here (fixed signs of (J), (D), and (B_z>0)), the skyrmion chirality is positive, and the electric field is not expected to alter it, as the magnetoelectric term is quadratic in the spin components."* 每个斯格明子近似携带 (Qapprox1)：*"each skyrmion carries an approximately quantized charge (Qapprox1), so that the total charge is proportional to their number."*

    • Baseline (E=0) phase sequence on square lattice

    固定 (J=D=1)；(D=1) 已知可稳定斯格明子：*"Throughout this work we set (J=1), which fixes the energy scale, and (D=1), known to stabilize skyrmions."* (E=0)、低温 (T=0.0009) 下：

    • (B_z=0.2)：spiral phase, Sp，单 (q) 双峰结构因子；原文：*"for magnetic field (B_z=0.2), the helical or spiral phase (Sp) is observed, characterized by its two peaks structure factor, known as single-q."*
    • (B_z=0.5)：SkX，三 (q) 六峰；*"for (B_z=0.5), the skyrmion crystal phase (SkX) emerges. Its structure factor, known as triple-q, displays six peaks."*
    • (B_z=0.9)：FM，横向结构因子近零；*"for (B_z=0.9), the ferromagnetic (FM) phase is reached, where spins align along the magnetic field direction and (ěcSₚₑᵣₚ(ěcq)approxěc0)."*
    • Thermally stabilized bimeron and skyrmion gas near phase boundaries

    有限温度在 Sp–SkX 间打开中间区，稳定 meron 激发并形成 bimeron phase：*"at any finite temperature, a gap opens at the Sp–SkX phase transition, in which excitations (merons) are stabilized, partially disrupting the spiral order and giving rise to the bimeron (Bm) phase, or 'elongated skyrmions'."* SkX–FM 过渡附近热涨落稳定 skyrmion gas：*"A similar argument applies to the SkX–FM transition, where thermal fluctuations stabilizes a skyrmion gas (SkG) phase, whose structure factor exhibits a ring-like pattern due to the absence of a preferred orientation."*

    ---

    III Low-temperature behaviour

    • Scope of low-temperature analysis

    低温部分分两层：先给两种电场方向的 ((B,E)) 相图、代表性自旋/偶极纹理和结构因子，再分析固定一个场、扫描另一个场时的序参量变化。原文：*"Subsection A presents and discusses the most relevant aspects of the phase diagrams for the two electric field directions, together with representative spin and dipole moment textures and their corresponding structure factors."* 以及：*"Subsection B focuses on the thermodynamic variables as functions of one of the fields, while fixing the other at characteristic values."*

    ---

    III.1 Phase diagrams in ((B,E))

    III.1.1 (ěcEparallel(1,1,0))

    • Competing alignment tendencies of (B_z) and in-plane electric field

    在 (Tapprox10⁻³)、(Eₓ₌E_y=Eₓy)、(E_z=0) 下绘制 (M_z,ḩi,Pₓy)。低 (Eₓy)、高 (B_z) 为 FM；高 (Eₓy)、低 (B_z) 时自旋趋向 ((1/2,1/2,1/sqrt2))，形成 FE 相。原文：*"At low (Eₓy) and high (B_z), the system is ferromagnetic (FM)."* 以及：*"at high (Eₓy) and low (B_z), the spins align in the ((1/2,1/2,1/sqrt2)) direction."* 该方向最小化单自旋磁电能 (happrox-(S^yS^z+S^xS^z)Eₓy)：*"This direction maximizes the polarization component parallel to the electric field, giving rise to a ferroelectric (FE) phase."*

    • In-plane electric field destroys SkX near (Eₓyapprox3)

    在零电场已存在斯格明子的中间磁场区域，增大 (Eₓy) 至约 3 会破坏斯格明子，但中间存在连续形变。原文：*"In the intermediate region, where skyrmions are present at zero electric field, applying (Eₓyapprox3) destroys the skyrmions, although intermediate deformations appear."*

    • SkX to SkG evolution at (B_z=0.4)

    (B_z=0.4) 下，(Eₓy=0.6) 时 SkX 的 triple-(q) 特征清晰；(Eₓy=2.0) 时晶格开始分离；(Eₓy=2.4) 时完全弥散为 SkG，背景自旋趋于平行并形成中心峰。原文：*"The triple-q structure of the SkX phase, well defined at (Eₓy=0.6), exhibits a central peak ... accompanied by secondary maxima."* 随后：*"At (Eₓy=2.0), the lattice begins to separate. At (Eₓy=2.4), the dispersion is complete and the central peak sharpens, corresponding to a SkG phase with a parallel-spin background."*

    • Chiral region shifts to lower magnetic fields under in-plane electric field

    (Eₓyapprox3) 会把手性区域推向更低 (B_z)，意味着电场可在更低磁场稳定斯格明子。原文：*"the competition between the fields also shifts the chiral region to lower magnetic fields for (Eₓyapprox3)."* 在 (B_z=0.2) 下，(Eₓy=2.8) 使螺旋相失稳并出现 skyrmion–bimeron 混合态；(Eₓy=3.2) 达到斯格明子数最大并形成 SkG；(Eₓyge3.8) 斯格明子减少并转向平行自旋相：*"At (B_z=0.2), increasing (Eₓy) up to 2.8 destabilizes the spiral phase, leading to a mixed state of skyrmions and bimerons."*；*"The number of skyrmions increases up to a maximum at (Eₓy=3.2), where a skyrmion gas forms."*；*"For (Eₓy≥3.8), the number of skyrmions decreases, giving way to the parallel-spin phase."*

    • Electric-field tunability as an experimentally relevant mechanism

    该低场稳定机制直接指向多铁体系中电场操控斯格明子相的实验可行性。原文：*"This result is particularly significant, as it demonstrates how the application of an electric field can effectively tune the chiral region, enabling the stabilization of skyrmions at lower magnetic fields."*

    • Single-skyrmion deformation at (B_z=0.5)

    孤立斯格明子在 (B_z=0.5)、(Eₓy=0,1,2) 下逐步解体。区域 B 自旋随 (Eₓy) 增大向 (xy) 平面倾斜；区域 A 向 (z) 方向倾斜；(Eₓy=2) 时区域 B 开始与背景融合。原文：*"The electric field competes with the magnetic field and DMI, progressively disassembling the skyrmion."*；*"Spins in region B tilt toward the xy-plane as (Eₓy) increases, while those in region A tilt toward the z direction."*；*"At (Eₓy=2), the region B starts merging with the background."*

    • Quadrupolar interpretation of deformation

    斯格明子—四极矩对应关系使形变可在偶极空间中直观看到：区域 B 中四极区域变大但强度减小，因为 (P_z) 分量减弱；区域 A 中 (P_z>0) 区域缩小。原文：*"the skyrmion–quadrupole relationship allows us to interpret this deformation more directly in dipolar-moment space."* 以及：*"in region B the quadrupole increases in size but decreases in intensity as the (P_z) component diminishes, while in region A the area with (P_z>0) is reduced."*

    • Finite-size robustness for in-plane field case

    (L=48,60,72) 的对比显示主要相图特征稳定。示例为 (Eₓy=0.6)、(B_z=0.4)，低温相为 SkX，具体热容和手性在三种尺寸下行为一致。原文：*"The results indicate that the main features of the phase diagram remain largely unchanged as the system size increases."*；*"The thermodynamic observables displayed (specific heat and chirality) exhibit a consistent behavior for (L=48,60,72)."*；*"These findings suggest that the observed phases and transitions are not dominated by finite-size effects."*

    ---

    III.1.2 (ěcEparallel(0,0,1))

    • Out-of-plane electric field favors (xy)-plane spin alignment at large (E_z)

    在 (Tapprox10⁻³)、(Eₓ₌E_y=0) 下，高 (B_z)、低 (E_z) 为 FM；低 (B_z)、高 (E_z) 时自旋趋向 ((1/sqrt2,1/sqrt2,0))，极化 (mathbf P=(0,0,1))。原文：*"At high (B_z) and low (E_z), the system is ferromagnetic (FM)."*；*"at low (B_z) and high (E_z), the spins align along ((1/sqrt2,1/sqrt2,0)) with (ěcP=(0,0,1))."* 该极限适用于 (E_zgg B_z,|J|,D)：*"minimizing the Hamiltonian when (E_zgg B_z, |J|, D)."*

    • Strong (E_z) destroys the chiral region, with lower critical (E_z) at lower (B_z)

    手性区域可被足够强的面外电场破坏，且低磁场下临界 (E_z) 更小。原文：*"The chiral region is destroyed by sufficiently strong electric fields, with the critical (E_z) decreasing at lower (B_z)."* 例如 (B_z=0.5) 下，(E_z=0.5) 的 SkX 在 (E_z=1.5) 转变为单 (q) 螺旋相：*"the SkX phase observed at (B_z=0.5), (E_z=0.5) ... evolves into a spiral phase with a single-q structure at (E_z=1.5)."*

    • Out-of-plane electric field creates chiral textures in otherwise ferromagnetic high-field region

    在 (B_zapprox0.8) 附近，(E=0) 基态为 FM；增大 (E_z) 后，手性相扩展到该高场区域。原文：*"In this region, the ground state at (E=0) is ferromagnetic; however, upon increasing (E_z), chiral phases extend into this field range."* 这说明电场能在本来共线的区域稳定非共线纹理：*"the application of an electric field stabilizes noncollinear textures in a parameter region that would otherwise be collinear."*

    • SkG to bimeron-rich to spiral sequence at (B_z=0.775)

    (B_z=0.775) 时，(E_zapprox2) 形成 SkG，斯格明子拉长并部分排列；结构因子为带峰的展宽环。(E_zapprox3) 进一步拉伸和不对称，进入 bimeron-rich 相；更大 (E_z) 下螺旋态主导并伴随宽 FM 区域。原文：*"for (B_z=0.775) and (E_zapprox2) we obtain a SkG phase in which skyrmions elongate and partially align."*；*"The corresponding (ěcSₚₑᵣₚ(ěcq)) shows a broadened ring with pronounced peaks, typical of elongated skyrmions."*；*"For (E_zapprox3), further elongation and asymmetry lead to a bimeron-rich (Bm) phase."*；*"At even larger (E_z), spiral states dominate, with wide ferromagnetic regions favored by the high (B_z)."*

    • Electric-field creation, annihilation, and deformation of skyrmions

    面外电场不仅破坏 SkX，还能在高磁场区域生成 SkG 与 Bm 结构。原文总结为：*"this result provides evidence of electric manipulation of magnetic structures, enabling the creation, annihilation, and deformation of skyrmions in new regions of magnetic field."*

    • Skyrmion elongation and dipole redistribution under (E_z)
